Abstract: We present the first approach to build hierarchical task-driven 3D scene graphs of arbitrary indoor or outdoor environments using an uncalibrated monocular camera in real-time. We leverage geometric foundation models to estimate geometric attributes of the scene graph (e.g., object bounding boxes), but we also observe that traversability information (the "places" layer of a scene graph) can be directly reconstructed by adding an extra head to existing geometric foundation models, like VGGT. Our approach is task-driven in the sense that we adjust the granularity of the objects and regions in the map depending on the task; for instance, during a manipulation task, our approach is able to resolve small knobs on a stove, while during a navigation task it can focus on large objects (e.g., the entire stove). However, in a major departure from related work, we consider the realistic case where the list of tasks is not predefined and fixed, but evolves as the robot operates. This naturally allows dealing with complex loco-manipulation tasks, where the robot can dynamically adjust its representation as the task unfolds. We dub the resulting approach FOUND-IT. FOUND-IT also includes an agentic approach to query information in the scene graph. In addition to achieving 79% higher accuracy on the ASHiTA SG3D task grounding benchmark, we demonstrate FOUND-IT runs in real-time on a ground robot using a Jetson Thor. Furthermore, to highlight the robustness of our method, we demonstrate constructing 3D scene graphs on casually captured realtor apartment tours from YouTube. Code will be made available upon publication.

</details>


### [131] [Decision-Making with Lightweight Confidence-Aware Language Model for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25393)
*Ruoyu Yao,Ruiguo Zhong,Pei Liu,Mingxing Peng,Rui Yang,Jun Ma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种轻量级置信感知语言模型框架，通过多智能体协作生成带置信标注的决策演示，并蒸馏到双头轻量模型，在nuPlan上实现SOTA成功率与低延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs/MLLMs在自动驾驶中具有巨大潜力，但其庞大的计算开销和高推理延迟严重阻碍了在资源受限系统中的部署。

Method: 设计多智能体协作流程（动作投票、置信评估、总结智能体）生成带置信标注的决策演示；通过置信感知微调策略和检索增强生成（RAG）将知识蒸馏到双头轻量语言模型，联合预测决策概率并生成文本推理过程。

Result: 在nuPlan基准的闭环实验中，在常规和长尾场景下均达到SOTA成功率，同时保持低推理延迟。

Conclusion: 提出的置信感知轻量语言模型框架有效弥合了复杂多模态意图推理与高效推理之间的差距，为资源受限的自动驾驶决策提供可行方案。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) and Multimodal LLMs (MLLMs) have demonstrated immense potential in autonomous driving (AD) by offering human-like reasoning and open-world generalization. However, the excessive computational overhead and high inference latency of these massive models severely hinder their deployment in resource-constrained AD systems. To address this challenge, we propose a novel decision-making framework utilizing a lightweight confidence-aware language model, which bridges the gap between complex multimodal intention reasoning and efficient inference. Specifically, we design a multi-agent collaborative workflow, comprising action voting, confidence assessment, and summarization agents, to generate high-quality, confidence-annotated decision demonstrations via explicit Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning. These demonstrations are then distilled into a lightweight language model featuring a dual-head architecture, enabling the joint prediction of decision probabilities and the generation of textual rationales. The distillation is realized via a confidence-aware fine-tuning strategy coupled with Retrieval Augmented Generation (RAG) to enhance the model's adaptability and data efficiency. Comprehensive closed-loop experiments on the nuPlan benchmark demonstrate that our approach achieves state-of-the-art (SOTA) success rates in both regular and long-tail scenarios while maintaining low inference latency.

</details>


### [132] [Path Following Control System of Line-of-Sight Guidance for Robotic Dolphin with Multi-Link Mechanism in Underwater Simulator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25401)
*Takumi Asada,Takao Oki,Hideo Furuhashi,Kenta Tabata,Renato Miyagusuku,Koichi Ozaki*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对具有多连杆机构的仿生自主水下航行器（BAUV）提出了一种路径跟踪系统，并在水下仿真中进行了评估，成功设计了适合BAUV的路径跟踪系统、使用模拟器确定了参数并评估了控制方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BAUV因低功耗和高机动性被广泛应用于水生生物观察和环境调查，但现有路径跟踪系统存在局限性，且针对多连杆机构BAUV的路径跟踪评估尚未明确，因此需要开发并验证适合BAUV的路径跟踪系统。

Method: 提出了一种适用于多连杆机构BAUV的路径跟踪系统，并在水下仿真环境中进行参数确定和控制方法评估。

Result: 成功设计了适合BAUV的路径跟踪系统，利用仿真器确定了系统参数，并评估了不同的控制方法。

Conclusion: 所提出的路径跟踪系统能够有效适用于多连杆机构BAUV，仿真验证了参数确定和控制方法评估的可行性。

Abstract: Biomimetic autonomous underwater vehicle (BAUV) with multi-link mechanism is widely used in aquatic life observation and environmental surveys due to its low power consumption and high maneuverability. An environmental survey requires a path following system that automatically follows specific points. However, the path following system of BAUV is limited, and its evaluation with multi-link mechanism robots has not yet been clarified. The path following system in BAUV requires prior simulation because the model differs depending on the type of biomimetics. In this study, we propose a path following system for BAUVs with a multi-link mechanism and evaluation in underwater simulation. In this result, it was possible to design a path following system suitable for BAUV, determine parameters using a simulator, and evaluate control methods.

</details>


### [133] [How to Mitigate the Distribution Shift Problem in Robotics Control: A Robust and Adaptive Approach Based on Offline to Online Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25414)
*Hyung-Suk Yoon,Seung-Woo Seo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种鲁棒的离线到自适应在线模仿学习框架，通过离线阶段利用辅助演示拓宽状态-动作覆盖范围，以及在线阶段检测分布偏移并进行自监督模仿学习，在MuJoCo环境中展现出优于基线算法的鲁棒性和适应性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模仿学习中的分布偏移问题导致智能体无法为训练中未访问的状态规划合适的动作，这主要源于专家演示相对于完整环境提供的固有狭窄状态-动作覆盖范围。

Method: 提出一个终身多阶段框架：离线学习阶段利用判别器有效训练策略以整合辅助演示，拓宽状态-动作覆盖范围；在线推理阶段检测分布偏移，通过在线经验进行自监督模仿学习以适应在线环境。

Result: 在MuJoCo环境中的广泛评估表明，该方法在分布偏移鲁棒性和在线环境适应性能方面均优于基线算法。

Conclusion: 该框架能够有效应对模仿学习中的分布偏移问题，展现出优越的性能。

Abstract: Distribution shift in imitation learning refers to the problem that the agent cannot plan proper actions for a state that has not been visited during the training. This problem can be largely attributed to the inherently narrow state-action coverage provided by expert demonstrations over the full environment. In this paper, we propose a robust offline to adaptive online imitation learning framework that handles the distribution shift problem in a lifelong, multi-phase scheme. In the offline learning phase, we leverage supplementary demonstrations to broaden the state-action coverage of the policy by utilizing a discriminator to effectively train the policy with supplementary demonstrations, thereby enhancing the robustness of the policy to distribution shift. In the subsequent online inference phase, our framework detects the occurrence of distribution shift and conducts self-supervised imitation learning from online experiences to adapt the policy to the online environments. Through extensive evaluations in MuJoCo environments, we demonstrate that our method exhibits better robustness to distribution shift and better adaptation performance to online environments than the baseline algorithms, which indicates superior performance of our framework against the distribution shift.

</details>


### [134] [OPAL: Omnidirectional Path-efficient Aerial 3D expLoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25423)
*Yoga Satwik Chappidi,Avideh Zakhor*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OPAL是一种用于无人机自主探索的框架，通过在分支点进行360度偏航旋转来替代计算密集的全局路径规划，结合LLM/VLM的前沿选择策略，在计算简单性和路径效率上优于EDEN、FALCON等基准方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人自主探索环境时，需要同时满足计算高效和行进距离短的需求。现有方法如全局路径规划计算复杂度过高，因此需要更轻量级的探索策略。

Method: OPAL框架在模糊分支点执行有意的360度偏航旋转（而非计算复杂的全局路径规划），旋转完成后进行前沿选择。提供了多种变体：无模型变体、基于LLM的变体、基于VLM的变体，并通过调整前沿搜索半径来权衡行进距离和总探索时间。

Result: 仿真结果表明：OPAL虽因原地偏航旋转增加了总探索时间，但计算更简单、行进距离更短、覆盖-距离曲线下面积更高。实际无人机实验中，OPAL变体行进距离比FALCON低25%。

Conclusion: 通过调整前沿选择搜索半径可以在行进距离和总探索时间之间取得权衡，OPAL在计算简单性和路径效率方面具有优势。

Abstract: Autonomous exploration is critical for robot mapping unknown environments. Desirable characteristics of exploration algorithms include compute efficiency and small traversed distance during the exploration process. Motivated by these, we present Omnidirectional Path-efficient Aerial 3D expLoration (OPAL), an exploration framework centered on deliberate 360-degree yaw rotation at ambiguous branch points rather than compute-heavy global tour planning. We devise multiple variants of OPAL to determine the frontier-selection strategy once the yaw pan is completed. One variant is model-free, while others use large language models (LLMs) or vision-language models (VLMs). We characterize the performance of these variants while varying the vicinity search radius to include frontiers in the selection process. Through simulations we find that although the time-consuming in-place yaw rotation increases total exploration time relative to more computationally complex baselines such as EDEN and FALCON, OPAL is computationally simpler and achieves shorter travel distances and higher coverage-versus-distance area under the curve. We also show that adjusting the frontier-selection search radius enables a tradeoff between travel distance and total exploration time. We verify our results on a Modal AI drone in two indoor environments by comparing OPAL against FALCON, and find that the traveled distance for a variant of OPAL to be as much as 25% lower than FALCON.

</details>


### [135] [EXPO-FT: Sample-Efficient Reinforcement Learning Finetuning for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25477)
*Perry Dong,Kuo-Han Hung,Tian Gao,Dorsa Sadigh,Chelsea Finn*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出EXPO-FT系统，通过强化学习微调预训练的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型，在约19.1分钟的在线机器人数据内实现多项高精度操作任务的完美成功率（30/30），显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型在机器人操作任务中展现出强大泛化能力，但预训练策略在真实部署中可靠性不足；强化学习微调虽有潜力，但现有方法要么从头训练未充分利用预训练先验，要么微调时样本效率和成功率不满足实际部署需求。

Method: 提出EXPO-FT系统，一种稳定、样本高效的强化学习微调方法，用于微调预训练的VLA策略。

Result: 在所有评估任务上实现完美任务性能（30/30成功率），平均仅需19.1分钟的在线机器人数据，在各项任务（穿灯串并插电、打台球入袋、插花入酒瓶）中均优于从头RL训练和VLA微调方法。

Conclusion: EXPO-FT成功弥合了VLA预训练策略与真实部署可靠性之间的差距，证明了高效RL微调VLA模型在机器人操作中的可行性和有效性。

Abstract: The ability to efficiently and reliably learn new tasks has been a foundational challenge in robotics. Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have demonstrated strong generalization across diverse manipulation tasks, yet pretrained policies consistently fall short of the reliability required for real-world deployment. Reinforcement learning (RL) fine-tuning offers a promising path to bridge this gap, but existing approaches either train from scratch without fully leveraging pretrained priors, or fine-tune VLAs without achieving the sample efficiency and success rates that practical deployment demands. We present EXPO-FT, a system for stable, sample-efficient RL finetuning of pretrained VLA policies that closes this gap. Our system solves a suite of challenging manipulation tasks, including routing string lights and inserting the plug to light it up, striking a pool ball into a pocket, and inserting a flower into a wine bottle, each requiring combinations of high precision, dynamic actions, and robustness to varied initial states. Our system achieves perfect task performance (30/30 successes) across all evaluated tasks within an average of 19.1 minutes of online robot data, outperforming both prior RL-from-scratch and VLA finetuning approaches. We release an open-source codebase with the aim of facilitating broader adoption of RL finetuning of VLA models in robotics.

</details>


### [136] [RepSAM: Bridging Foundation Models to Robotic Vision via Representation-Guided Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25495)
*Wenhui Chu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RepSAM提出了一种表示引导的参数高效微调框架，通过CKA引导的秩分配策略和多模态融合模块，在机器人视觉任务中以158倍参数减少实现了全微调性能的97.9%（89.0% vs 90.9% mIoU），且训练时间从384 GPU-hours降至4小时。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人感知在非结构化环境中仍具有挑战性，研究发现基础模型（如SAM）在跨Transformer层存在非均匀表示偏移：浅层领域差距大（CKA < 0.5），深层迁移效果好（CKA > 0.7），这导致了性能下降。

Method: 提出RepSAM框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) 基于CKA引导的秩分配策略，根据各层表示迁移性差异动态分配LoRA秩；(2) 多模态融合模块，增强对透明物体和杂乱场景等挑战性机器人场景的鲁棒处理能力。

Result: 在6个基准和机器人操作任务中，RepSAM以4.0M可训练参数（全微调632M的1/158）达到89.0% mIoU（全微调90.9%），训练仅需4小时（全微调384 GPU-hours的1/96），操作成功率比LoRA(RGB)基线提升12.0%，所有改进统计显著（p < 0.01）。

Conclusion: RepSAM验证了考虑层间表示迁移性差异进行参数高效微调的有效性，为将基础模型高效适配到机器人视觉任务提供了新范式，在显著降低计算和参数开销的同时保持了接近全微调的性能水平。

Abstract: Robotic perception in unstructured environments remains challenging despite the zero-shot capabilities of foundation models such as SAM. This work attributes performance degradation to non-uniform representation shifts across transformer layers: shallow layers exhibit substantial domain gaps (CKA < 0.5), whereas deep layers transfer effectively (CKA > 0.7). Based on this observation, we propose RepSAM, a representation-guided parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) framework for adapting foundation models to robotic vision. RepSAM employs a theoretically grounded CKA-guided rank allocation strategy combined with a multi-modal fusion module for robust handling of challenging robotic scenarios, including transparent objects and cluttered scenes. Experimental evaluation across six benchmarks and robotic manipulation tasks demonstrates that RepSAM achieves 97.9% of full fine-tuning performance (89.0% vs. 90.9% mIoU) while reducing trainable parameters by 158x (from 632M to 4.0M). RepSAM outperforms DoRA by 7.9% mIoU with just 4 hours of training on a single A100 GPU (a 96x reduction from full fine-tuning, which takes 384 GPU-hours). These improvements are statistically significant (p < 0.01) and translate to a 12.0% absolute improvement in robotic manipulation success rates over the LoRA (RGB) baseline.

</details>


### [137] [Action-Prior Denoising for Smooth Real-Time Chunking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25537)
*Dongyang Liu,Zhaowen Zheng,Yu Sun,Longxu Zhang,Yixuan Liu,Hao Wan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Soft RTC方法，通过在训练时使用部分去噪状态而非纯噪声来构造重叠token，实现更平滑的动作过渡，在保持近零推理开销的同时降低高延迟下的动作突变和抖动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的训练时实时分块（RTC）方法使用二进制前缀掩码，将所有非前缀token视为完全无约束，未能充分建模异步执行场景：早期重叠动作是固定的，而后期重叠动作虽可编辑但仍应接近先前计划。

Method: 提出Soft RTC，一种基于动作先验去噪的训练时RTC泛化方法。通过从部分去噪状态而非纯噪声构造损坏的重叠token，并在推理时通过轻量级token级混合规则注入对齐的先前块作为相同先验。

Result: 在12个Kinetix关卡上，短软窗口接近硬训练时RTC的总体解决率（0.809 vs 0.815），中窗口将高延迟下的动作差和抖动分别降低9.1%和9.6%。两种变体均保持近朴素推理时间。初步真实机器人分拣实验也表明Soft RTC提供了最低的命令动作有限差分指标。

Conclusion: Soft RTC通过软约束更有效地建模了异步执行中的动作重叠关系，在保持低推理开销的同时显著改善了高延迟下的动作平滑度，验证了训练时RTC在真实机器人任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Real-time chunking (RTC) lets chunked action policies operate under inference delay by conditioning a newly generated action chunk on actions already committed by the previous chunk. Training-time RTC simulates this delay during learning and avoids expensive guidance at deployment, but its binary prefix mask treats all non-prefix tokens as fully unconstrained. This under-models asynchronous execution: early overlap actions are fixed, while later overlap actions remain editable but should still stay close to the previous plan. We propose Soft RTC, a training-time RTC generalization based on action-prior denoising. Soft RTC constructs corrupted overlap tokens from partially denoised states instead of pure noise and injects the aligned previous chunk as the same prior during inference through a lightweight token-wise blending rule. On the 12 released large Kinetix levels, a short soft window nearly matches hard training-time RTC in overall solve rate (0.809 vs. 0.815), while a medium window reduces high-delay action delta and jerk by 9.1% and 9.6% relative to hard RTC. Both variants keep near-naive runtime, unlike inference-time RTC baselines. A small preliminary real-robot sorting study provides additional evidence that training-time RTC can improve completion and that Soft RTC gives the lowest commanded-action finite-difference metrics among the tested policies.

</details>


### [138] [Safety-Critical Whole-Body Control for Humanoid Robots via Input-to-State Safe Control Barrier Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25546)
*Kwanwoo Lee,Sanghyuk Park,Gyeongjae Park,Myeong-Ju Kim,Jaeheung Park*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于输入-状态安全控制屏障函数（ISSf-CBF）的分层安全关键全身控制框架，通过集成运动学级全身控制器、ISSf-CBF安全滤波器和动力学级全身控制器，在存在未知扰动的情况下保障人形机器人的运动学安全约束，实验验证了其在模型失配下的安全裕度提升和实时多约束满足能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全关键控制方法在人形机器人实际运行中，由于模型不确定性、轨迹跟踪误差和外部扰动等未知干扰的存在，运动学安全保障会退化。需要一种能够在扰动下仍能维持安全约束的控制框架。

Method: 提出分层安全关键全身控制框架：1) 运动学级全身控制器（KinWBC）生成名义关节运动参考；2) ISSf-CBF安全滤波器在受界扰动下最小化修改参考以满足运动学安全约束；3) 动力学级全身控制器（DynWBC）跟踪滤波后参考并保证全身体动态可行性和接触稳定性。通过保守调参将运动学安全保证传递到全阶人形动力学。

Result: 仿真和实际机器人实验表明，该框架在模型失配下提升了安全裕度，能够在行走、遥操作、单腿平衡及手部控制等任务中实时可靠地执行多种安全约束。

Conclusion: 基于ISSf-CBF的分层控制框架能有效应对未知扰动对人形机器人安全约束的威胁，实现了运动学安全保证向完整动力学的传递，并通过实验验证了其有效性和实时性。

Abstract: Safety-critical control is essential for humanoid robots operating in complex human-centered environments, where physical safety constraints such as joint limits, self-collision avoidance, obstacle avoidance, and workspace boundaries must be satisfied during real-robot operation. However, existing approaches remain limited because kinematic safety guarantees can be degraded in the presence of unknown disturbances, such as model uncertainties, trajectory-tracking errors, and external perturbations. This paper presents a hierarchical safety-critical whole-body control framework for humanoid robots based on input-to-state safe control barrier functions (ISSf-CBFs). The proposed architecture integrates a kinematic-level whole-body controller (KinWBC), an ISSf-CBF safety filter, and a dynamic-level whole-body controller (DynWBC). KinWBC generates nominal joint-motion references from prioritized tasks; the ISSf-CBF filter minimally modifies these references to satisfy kinematic safety constraints under bounded disturbances; and DynWBC tracks the filtered references while enforcing full-body dynamic feasibility and contact stability. Safety constraints are imposed on a whole-body kinematic model, and the ISSf-CBF parameters are conservatively tuned so that the resulting kinematic safety guarantees can be transferred to full-order humanoid dynamics under unknown disturbances. Simulation and real-robot experiments demonstrate that the proposed framework improves safety margins under model mismatch and reliably enforces multiple safety constraints in real time during locomotion, teleoperation, and single-leg balancing with hand control. Project website: https://kwlee365.github.io/SafeWBC-Website/

</details>


### [139] [TapSampling: Inference-Time Sampling with a Task-Progress-Understanding Verifier for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25547)
*Sizhe Zhao,Shengping Zhang,Shuo Yang,Weiyu Zhao,Shuigen Wang,Xiangyang Ji*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出TapSampling，一个即插即用的推理时采样框架，通过Action-VAE将动作压缩到低维潜空间进行多次采样，并利用任务进度预测器进行动作验证，在不微调策略的情况下提升多种具身控制策略的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身控制研究主要通过扩大训练数据和模型规模来提升性能，而忽略了推理时策略这一替代维度。非确定性生成模型（如扩散模型、自回归模型）虽被广泛采用，但单次推理范式限制了其性能表现。

Method: 1) Action-VAE：将策略生成的初始动作映射到低维潜空间的压缩后验分布，从中采样任意数量的潜变量并解码为候选动作；2) 动作验证模块：利用机器人数据集的时序结构训练任务进度预测器，作为可解释的动作选择验证器；3) 即插即用、策略无关的推理时采样框架。

Result: 在仿真和真实环境的广泛实验中，TapSampling在不进行策略微调的情况下显著提升了多种通用策略的性能。

Conclusion: 推理时采样是一个有效的性能提升维度，TapSampling作为策略无关的插件框架，能够通过潜空间采样和语义验证机制增强现有具身控制策略。

Abstract: Existing embodied control research demonstrates remarkable performance improvements by scaling training data and model size. We instead explore inference-time strategy as an alternative axis. Non-deterministic generative models, such as diffusion and autoregressive models, have been widely adopted in the field of embodied control. However, the single-shot inference paradigm limits their performance. In this paper, we propose \textbf{TapSampling}, a plug-and-play framework for inference-time sampling. First, we introduce an Action-VAE that represents actions in a low-dimensional latent space by mapping policy-generated initial actions into a compressed posterior distribution, from which any number of latent samples can be drawn and decoded into candidate actions that approximate the true action distribution. Second, we formulate action verification as task-progress outcome prediction, using the intrinsic sequential structure of robotic datasets to train a semantically grounded verifier for interpretable action selection. Furthermore, TapSampling is a policy-agnostic framework. Extensive experiments in both simulated and real-world environments demonstrate that our method substantially improves multiple generalist policies without further policy finetuning. Code and models are available at the project page.

</details>


### [140] [Acting on the Unseen: Communication-Free Collaborative Filtering for Decentralized Multi-Robot Task Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25584)
*Alexander Apartsin,Yigal Meshulam,Yehudit Aperstein*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出零知识多机器人任务分配（ZK-MRTA）问题，机器人在无先验知识、无通信、仅有部分噪声观测的条件下，通过在线低秩协同过滤（SwarmCF）从公开的队友结果流中学习，实现了对未曾尝试任务的零样本泛化，理论证明其样本复杂度为Θ(d)而非Θ(n)，并实验验证了80%的集中式全通信性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MRTA研究通常假设通信、已知任务模型或协调器存在，但实际中常出现相反极端情况：机器人无先验知识、无通信、仅能观察部分噪声结果。作者填补了这一理论空白，定义ZK-MRTA问题。

Method: 提出SwarmCF算法：机器人各自运行在线低秩协同过滤，从公开广播的队友结果流中学习，利用隐藏的低秩结构推断未尝试任务的适配度，实现零样本推理和新任务接入。

Result: 理论证明：SwarmCF的每机器人样本复杂度为Θ(d)（d为秩），无结构学习器为Θ(n)（n为任务数），两者存在类别性差距而非常数因子；在任务稀缺场景下实现任意时间的累积奖励分离；给出精确恢复的确定性条件。实验表明：广播有价值，未见对技能随团队规模正相关，恢复约80%的集中式全通信性能，在容量1争用和机器人感知实例中保持鲁棒。

Conclusion: 低秩结构是解决ZK-MRTA问题的关键假设，SwarmCF无需任何先验知识或通信即可高效学习，且团队规模扩大带来正扩展律，为实际无通信多机器人系统提供了理论支撑和可行方案。

Abstract: Multi-robot task allocation usually assumes some combination of communication, known task models, or a coordinator. We study the opposite extreme, a regime common in practice but overlooked in theory, which we name Zero-Knowledge MRTA (ZK-MRTA): a robot team with no prior knowledge (no task models, not even the latent rank), no communication (no messages, no parameter sharing, no coordinator), and only a partial and privately-noisy view of a public stream of teammates' outcomes. A hidden low-rank structure governs which robot suits which task, and there are far more tasks than rounds, so most (robot, task) pairs are never attempted. Yet each robot can act well on tasks it never attempted, and onboard new tasks, by running online low-rank collaborative filtering over the broadcast (SwarmCF). The advantage over any structure-free learner is categorical, not a constant factor: a structure-free learner is provably at the prior-mean error floor on unseen pairs. We prove a matching per-robot sample complexity (Θ(d) versus Θ(n), in the rank d and the task count n), an anytime (cumulative-reward) separation under task scarcity, and a deterministic condition under which decentralized recovery from the masked broadcast is exact (validated empirically). Experiments quantify the value of the broadcast, a positive scaling law (per-robot unseen-pair skill rises with team size), and the strongest masking-robustness and anytime profile among low-rank methods, recovering most (about 80% on earned skill) of a centralized full-communication ceiling, and holding under capacity-1 contention and in a robotics-grounded sensing instance.

</details>


### [141] [Compliant Non-Prehensile Pushing Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25672)
*Francesco Cufino,Mario Selvaggio,Fabio Amadio,Fabio Ruggiero*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将非抓取推动操作与阻抗控制机器人相结合的方法，通过模型预测控制和能量罐无源滤波器，实现了在人类环境中的安全、柔顺推动操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在人类共存环境中，机器人需要具备柔顺性以应对外部物理交互。非抓取推动操作中的外力干扰会导致跟踪误差和推动力无限增长，缺乏安全保障机制。

Method: 1) 扩展现有的推动模型以适配阻抗控制机器人；2) 构建基于该模型的模型预测控制框架，通过优化位置/速度设定点实现柔顺推动；3) 集成能量罐无源滤波器，调节机器人速度设定点以保证无源性，防止能量不受控积累。

Result: 在仿真和两个不同机器人系统上进行了实验验证，证明了人机交互中的被动柔顺性，评估了轨迹跟踪性能和对物体物理参数变化的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该方法有效实现了柔顺非抓取推动操作，能够在人机交互中保持被动性，防止外力干扰导致的能量失控问题，具有较好的鲁棒性。

Abstract: In this paper, we address the challenge of performing non-prehensile pushing operations with a compliant robotic manipulation system. To ensure safe operations in human-populated environments, robots must comply with external physical interactions and exhibit passive behavior. To achieve this, we extend a state-of-the-art pushing model to integrate it with impedance-controlled robots. We develop a model predictive control framework built upon this model that enables compliant pushing through optimal modulation of the robot's position/velocity set-point, jointly realizing the required pushing force and contact point adaptation to obtain desired object motion. However, external interactions may induce tracking errors, causing a consequent potentially indefinite increase of the pushing force. To prevent this, we integrate an energy tank passivity filter that further modulates the robot velocity set-point to guarantee passivity and avoid uncontrolled energy buildup. The proposed method has been rigorously tested in simulation and validated through experiments on two different robotic systems, demonstrating passive compliance during human-robot interactions and assessing trajectory tracking performance and robustness to variations in the object's physical parameters.

</details>


### [142] [HumanFlow -- Diffusion-Driven MAV Navigation Among Humans via Tightly-Coupled Motion Tracking, Forecasting, and Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25685)
*Simon Schaefer,Joshua Näf,Stefan Leutenegger*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HumanFlow是一种潜扩散模型，统一了人体运动跟踪与预测，并基于3D场景上下文进行条件生成。在严重遮挡等挑战性条件下，该模型能生成平滑准确的运动预测，在跟踪精度上超越当前最优方法且效率更高。同时，其潜空间可与基于流匹配的近似MPC策略紧密结合，用于MAV社会导航，在部分可观测性下实现无碰撞的优越导航性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在严重遮挡或部分可见情况下，无法预测与周围场景一致的合理且准确的人体运动估计，限制了机器人在日常环境中运行的安全性和效率。

Method: 提出HumanFlow潜扩散模型，以3D场景上下文为条件，统一人体运动跟踪与预测。其潜空间与基于流匹配的近似MPC控制策略紧密耦合。

Result: 在严重遮挡等挑战条件下生成平滑准确预测；在跟踪精度上超越当前最优方法且效率更高；在MAV社会导航仿真中，即使部分可观测情况下也能实现无碰撞的优越导航性能。

Conclusion: HumanFlow通过将3D场景上下文化的人体运动模型与控制策略结合，有效提升了机器人在复杂场景下对人类的感知与导航能力。

Abstract: Robust and accurate perception of humans in their 3D scene context is essential for integrating robots into everyday environments. Existing approaches, however, often fail to predict plausible and accurate human motion estimates that are consistent with the surrounding scene, especially in the presence of heavy occlusions or partial visibility. This can limit both safety and efficiency for robotic operations. We introduce HumanFlow, a latent diffusion model that unifies human motion tracking and forecasting, conditioned on the 3D scene context. We show that our human motion model produces smooth and accurate predictions under challenging conditions, including heavy occlusions, and outperforms state-of-the-art methods in tracking accuracy while being significantly more efficient. Furthermore, we show how HumanFlow's latent space can be tightly coupled with control by conditioning a flow-matching-based, approximate MPC policy on these representations. We validate our policy in simulation with real human trajectories for MAV social navigation, demonstrating superior navigation performance and remaining collision-free, even under partial observability of the human.

</details>


### [143] [Implicit Null-space Manifold Generation for Redundant Robotic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25770)
*Taiki Ishigaki,Teresa Vidal-Calleja,Ko Ayusawa,Eiichi Yoshida*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于隐式标量场的表示学习方法，通过雅可比引导的探索策略估计冗余机器人系统解流形的几何结构，并在平面三连杆和七自由度Franka机械臂上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常通过雅可比技术局部利用冗余度计算单个解或轨迹，但无法保留解集本身的几何结构表示。

Method: 构建定义在构型空间上的隐式标量场，其零水平集对应解流形；采用雅可比引导的探索策略在解流形邻域生成样本，高效捕获局部和全局结构。

Result: 在平面三连杆机器人和七自由度Franka机械臂上实验验证了所提表示的有效性。

Conclusion: 该框架能够对解空间进行连续建模，适用于连续变化的任务族。

Abstract: Robotic systems with redundant degrees of freedom can achieve the same task outcome using multiple configurations, resulting in solution sets that form manifolds in the configuration space. Existing approaches typically exploit such redundancy locally through Jacobian-based techniques to compute individual solutions or trajectories. While effective for solution computation, these methods do not retain a representation of the geometry of the solution set itself. In this work, we adopt a representation-centric approach to estimate the geometric structure of the solution space. We consider solution manifolds induced by general task-defining maps and construct an implicit scalar field over the configuration space, whose zero-level set corresponds to the solution manifold. To this end, we generate samples in the neighborhood of the solution manifold using a Jacobian-guided exploration strategy, which efficiently captures its local and global structure. The resulting implicit representation is defined over the configuration space and naturally induces a continuous, distance field that encodes proximity to the solution manifold. Experiments on a planar three-link robot and a seven-degree-of-freedom Franka manipulator demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed representation. Furthermore, the framework enables consistent modeling of solution spaces across families of tasks with continuous variation.

</details>


### [144] [ParkourFormer: Integrating Predictive Supervision and Sequence Modeling into Parkour Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25782)
*Yanheng Mai,Wenhao Xu,Zirui Huang,Yifei Fu,Shengwei Dong,Xinjue Wang,Kailun Huang,Yanzhe Xie,Renjing Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出ParkourFormer，一种基于Transformer的序列建模框架，通过显式预测短期未来本体感受状态来增强人形机器人跑酷运动策略，在多种复杂地形上实现了93.85%的平均穿越成功率，相比MLP、MoE-MLP和普通Transformer基线方法提升高达42.73%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习策略 largely 是反应式的，直接将观测映射到动作，没有显式建模未来身体状态。在敏捷运动任务中，成功执行高度依赖于预期即将到来的接触转换和身体动力学，因此需要一种能够联合推理运动历史和预期未来动态的策略。

Method: ParkourFormer采用Transformer序列建模框架，通过交叉注意力机制使当前机器人状态查询历史传感运动轨迹，同时使用轻量级预测头预测短期未来本体感受状态（通过监督信号训练），将预测的未来状态与时间特征融合生成动作。

Result: 在模拟和真实人形机器人上，ParkourFormer在高度挑战性地形上达到93.85%平均穿越成功率，相比MLP、MoE-MLP和普通Transformer基线方法提升高达42.73%，且使用单一统一策略覆盖所有地形类型。

Conclusion: 显式未来状态建模能够显著提高敏捷全身运动控制的鲁棒性和泛化能力，Transformer序列建模框架可以有效解决人形机器人跑酷中的未来条件决策问题。

Abstract: Humanoid parkour requires locomotion policies to coordinate whole-body dynamics across rapidly changing terrains such as stairs, gaps, slopes, and obstacles. Existing reinforcement learning policies are largely reactive, mapping observations directly to actions without explicitly modeling future body states. Such modeling becomes critical in agile locomotion tasks where successful motion execution depends strongly on anticipating upcoming contact transitions and body dynamics.We present ParkourFormer, a Transformer-based sequence modeling framework that reformulates humanoid locomotion as a future-conditioned decision-making problem. The current robot state queries historical sensorimotor trajectories through cross-attention, while a lightweight prediction head forecasts short-horizon future proprioceptive states. The predicted future states, trained with supervised signals, are fused with temporal features to generate actions, enabling the policy to jointly reason over motion history and anticipated future dynamics. We evaluate ParkourFormer on a diverse multi-terrain humanoid parkour benchmark including stairs, gaps, slopes, rough terrain, and obstacle traversal. Experiments in simulation and on a real humanoid robot show that ParkourFormer achieves a 93.85% average traversal success rate on highly challenging terrains, with improvements of up to 42.73% over strong MLP, MoE-based MLP, and vanilla Transformer baselines, while maintaining a single unified policy across all terrain types. These results demonstrate that explicit future-state modeling significantly improves robustness and generalization for agile whole-body locomotion.

</details>


### [145] [HoLoArm: Deformable Arms for Collision-Tolerant Quadrotor Flight](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25790)
*Quang Ngoc Pham,Jonas Eschmann,Yang Zhou,Alejandro Ojeda Olarte,Giuseppe Loianno,Van Anh Ho*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了HoLoArm，一种受蜻蜓翅膀节点结构启发的四旋翼无人机，其具备柔性臂设计，结合强化学习控制策略，能够在碰撞后0.3-0.6秒内恢复，承受高达7.6 m/s的撞击速度，并携带540g有效载荷稳定飞行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机在人类密集环境中应用增多，需要能够承受碰撞并快速恢复的设计，以降低对人和环境的风险。

Method: 受蜻蜓翅膀节点结构启发设计柔性臂（HoLoArm），提供自然柔韧性和弹性；集成强化学习控制策略增强恢复和悬停性能。

Result: HoLoArm可在任意方向（包括轴向）被动变形，根据撞击方向和程度在0.3-0.6秒内恢复；能承受高达7.6 m/s的碰撞速度，携带540g有效载荷并保持稳定飞行。

Conclusion: 该工作为具有高敏捷性和可靠安全性的软体空中机器人形态设计做出贡献，支持在杂乱和人类共享环境中运行，并为未来将柔性结构与智能控制相结合的完全软体无人机奠定基础。

Abstract: The increasing use of drones in human-centric applications highlights the need for designs that can survive collisions and recover rapidly, minimizing risks to both humans and the environment. We present HoLoArm, a quadrotor with compliant arms inspired by the nodus structure of dragonfly wings. This design provides natural flexibility and resilience while preserving flight stability, which is further reinforced by the integration of a Reinforcement Learning (RL) control policy that enhances both recovery and hovering performance. Experimental results demonstrate that HoLoArm can passively deform in any direction, including axial one, and recover within 0.3-0.6 s depending on the direction and level of the impact. The drone can survive collisions at speeds up to 7.6 m/s and carry a 540 g payload while maintaining stable flight. This work contributes to the morphological design of soft aerial robots with high agility and reliable safety, enabling operation in cluttered and human shared environments, and lays the groundwork for future fully soft drones that integrate compliant structures with intelligent control.

</details>


### [146] [Extending Embodied Question Answering from Perception to Decision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25813)
*Xicheng Gong,Qiwei Li,Peiran Xu,Yadong Mu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了EQA-Decision，一个覆盖静态场景构建、空间理解、任务动态推理和即时决策四个维度的超大规模具身问答数据集（400万+ QA对），并构建了RoboDecision基线模型，用于统一评估具身环境中的感知、推理和动作级决策能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身问答数据集和基准碎片化，各自只关注有限的推理技能（如空间理解或程序性推理），缺乏统一的、大规模的综合评估框架。

Method: 构建了EQA-Decision数据集，系统覆盖四个互补的具身推理维度（静态场景构建、空间理解、任务动态推理、即时决策），包含超过400万个问答对及层次化标注；同时开发了RoboDecision基线模型，提供统一的评估框架。

Result: EQA-Decision有效基准测试并增强了VLM在空间和交互推理方面的能力，为推进具身智能研究提供了坚实基础。

Conclusion: EQA-Decision作为一个大规模、多维度统一的具身问答基准，能够系统评估和提升视觉语言模型在具身环境中的感知-推理-决策全链条能力。

Abstract: Embodied Question Answering (EQA) connects perception, reasoning, and interaction within embodied environments. However, existing datasets and benchmarks remain fragmented, each focusing on a limited subset of reasoning skills such as spatial understanding or procedural reasoning, without offering a unified large-scale framework for comprehensive evaluation. We present EQA-Decision, a large-scale embodied QA dataset that systematically covers four complementary dimensions of embodied reasoning: static scene construction, spatial understanding, task dynamics reasoning, and instant decision. The dataset contains over four million question-answer pairs with hierarchical annotations across diverse embodied scenarios. In addition, we develop RoboDecision, a strong baseline model aligned with the EQA-Decision Benchmark, providing a unified framework that jointly evaluates perception, reasoning, and action-level decision-making in embodied environments. Results demonstrate that EQA-Decision effectively benchmarks and enhances VLM capabilities in spatial and interaction reasoning, providing a solid foundation for advancing embodied intelligence research.

</details>


### [147] [OASIS: Observation-Action Space Alignment via SE(3) Trajectory Prediction for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25829)
*Xinzhe Chen,Sihua Ren,Liqi Huang,Haowen Sun,Mingyang Li,Xingyu Chen,Zeyang Liu,Xuguang Lan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OASIS是一种视觉运动策略，通过SE(3)末端执行器轨迹预测将中间表示与动作空间对齐，在仿真和真实实验中优于现有VLA和WAM基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型和WAM的中间表示停留在观测空间，缺乏动作空间的刚体几何结构，迫使动作解码器隐式恢复该几何结构，导致性能受限。

Method: OASIS结合3D感知特征编码器（融合视觉-语言和度量深度特征）与SE(3)轨迹预测器（预测相机帧末端执行器轨迹），动作解码器基于预测器位姿监督的隐藏状态生成与刚体运动一致的动作块。

Result: 在仿真和真实实验中，OASIS在成功率和分布外泛化方面均优于VLA和WAM基线方法。

Conclusion: 将中间表示通过SE(3)轨迹预测显式对齐到动作空间的刚体几何结构，能有效提升机器人操作策略的性能和泛化能力。

Abstract: Recent vision-language-action (VLA) models and world action models (WAMs) advance robotic manipulation by enriching intermediate representations with auxiliary spatial features or future visual-state prediction. However, these representations largely remain within the observation space and do not share the rigid-body geometry of the action space, forcing the action decoder to implicitly recover this geometry. We propose OASIS, a visuomotor policy that aligns the intermediate representation with the action space via $SE(3)$ end-effector trajectory prediction. OASIS couples a 3D-aware feature encoder that fuses vision-language and metric-depth features with an $SE(3)$ trajectory predictor that produces a camera-frame end-effector trajectory. Conditioned on the predictor's pose-supervised hidden states, the action decoder generates action chunks consistent with rigid-body motion. Across simulation and real-world experiments, OASIS outperforms VLA and WAM baselines in success rate and out-of-distribution generalization. Our project page is available at https://npuhandsome.github.io/OASIS_web.

</details>


### [148] [When Search Becomes Memory: Turning Robot Design Trials into Transferable Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25832)
*Yunfei Wang,Xiaohao Xu,Yang Li,Xiaonan Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Auto-Robotist是一个自我进化的大语言模型智能体，它将形态搜索轨迹蒸馏为显式的自然语言技能库，使设计记忆可检查、可复用，在EvoGym任务中显著提升了冷启动搜索和技能迁移效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于LLM的进化机器人设计循环是无记忆的——模拟结果用于塑造下一代种群，但不作为可复用的设计知识保留。

Method: 提出Auto-Robotist，一个自进化LLM智能体。它将形态搜索轨迹蒸馏为显式自然语言技能库（包含结构原型、正负规则及支撑设计），搜索时检索技能以条件化LLM编辑精英个体，同时保留GA变异路径进行探索；评估后通过添加、诊断和合并操作更新技能库。

Result: 在七个EvoGym任务上，Auto-Robotist改进了冷启动5x5搜索，并将习得技能迁移到10x10设计空间，参考条件化迁移在所有任务上优于纯GA。

Conclusion: LLM智能体能够将昂贵的物理评估转化为可复用、可审计的设计原则。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used as proposal generators for evolutionary robot design, yet most loops remain memoryless: simulator results shape the next population but are not preserved as reusable design knowledge. We present Auto-Robotist, a self-evolving LLM agent that distills morphology-search traces into an explicit natural-language skill library. Each skill stores a structural archetype, evidence-grounded positive and negative rules, and the evaluated designs that support them, making design memory inspectable rather than implicit in a population. During search, the agent retrieves skills to condition LLM edits of elite bodies while retaining a Genetic Algorithm (GA) mutation path for exploration; after evaluation, it updates the library through Add, Diagnose, and Merge. Across seven EvoGym tasks spanning locomotion, traversal, and object interaction, Auto-Robotist improves cold-start 5x5 search and transfers learned skills to 10x10 design spaces, where reference-conditioned transfer outperforms GA on every task. These results suggest that LLM agents can convert expensive physical evaluations into reusable, auditable design principles. Our code will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [149] [RePlan-Bot: Multi-Level Replanning for Embodied Instruction Following](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25851)
*Xicheng Gong,Guozheng Sun,Peiran Xu,Yadong Mu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出RePlan-Bot，一种通过多层级持续重规划策略（高层LLM审计器动态调整子目标、常识引导搜索定位物体、轻量ViT修正器预防风险动作）在ALFRED基准上实现具身指令跟踪SOTA性能的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身指令跟踪方法在长程规划和处理不可逆状态变化时表现不佳，导致任务成功率低。

Method: RePlan-Bot包含三个核心组件：1）基于LLM的高层审计器，根据环境反馈动态调整子目标；2）基于多层实例图的常识引导搜索机制，实现精确结构化的物体定位；3）轻量级ViT修正器，提前修正高风险的低层动作。

Result: 在ALFRED基准的已知和未知环境中均达到SOTA性能。

Conclusion: 多层级持续重规划策略能显著提升具身指令跟踪的适应性和可靠性。

Abstract: Embodied instruction following (EIF) requires agents to understand and execute complex natural language commands within interactive 3D environments. Despite recent advances, existing methods often fail in long-horizon planning and handling irreversible state changes, resulting in low task success rates. To address these challenges, we introduce RePlan-Bot, a novel EIF agent that performs multi-level, continuous replanning throughout task execution. RePlan-Bot integrates a high-level LLM-based auditor for dynamic sub-goal adjustments guided by environmental feedback, a commonsense-guided search mechanism based on a multi-layered instance map for precise and structured object localization, and a lightweight ViT-based corrector to preemptively fix risky low-level actions. Evaluated on the ALFRED benchmark, RePlan-Bot achieves state-of-the-art performance in both seen and unseen environments, demonstrating superior adaptability and reliability.

</details>


### [150] [AnyScene: Towards Highly Controllable Driving Scene Generation at Anywhere and Beyond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26113)
*Haiming Zhang,Junfei Zhou,Feng Jiang,Jingzhong Li,Zhenglong Guo,Penglin Dai,Jifeng Dai,Yan Xie,Benjin Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AnyScene提出了一种以占据栅格为核心的统一框架，通过时空占据扩散Transformer从BEV布局生成语义占据序列，再基于几何引导的视角扩展模块合成多视角驾驶视频，实现了高保真、可控制的驾驶场景生成，在占据和视频生成上达到SOTA，并支持跨数据集和用户自定义布局。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有占据引导方法依赖浅层条件机制和参考帧相关的视频合成，限制了从任意BEV布局进行细粒度控制的能力，难以用于可扩展的仿真，尤其是长尾安全关键场景的生成。

Method: 提出AnyScene框架，包含两个核心模块：(1) 时空占据扩散Transformer，以自回归方式联合tokenize BEV和占据特征，从BEV布局生成语义占据序列；(2) 几何引导的视角扩展模块，将占据作为规范空间表示，以无参考、自回归方式合成时序一致的多视角驾驶视频，支持灵活相机配置。

Result: 在占据生成和视频生成任务上均达到SOTA性能；对未见和自定义布局展现出强泛化能力；为下游任务（如稀疏视角3D重建）提供可衡量的性能提升。

Conclusion: AnyScene通过占据作为中间表示的范式，实现了从BEV布局到多视角视频的端到端可控生成，为自动驾驶仿真中长尾场景的可扩展生成提供了一种统一有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Generating high-fidelity and controllable synthetic data is critical for advancing end-to-end autonomous driving, particularly for addressing the long tail of rare safety-critical scenarios. Existing occupancy-guided methods typically rely on shallow conditioning mechanisms and reference-frame-dependent video synthesis, which limits fine-grained controllability from arbitrary BEV layouts and restricts their applicability for scalable simulation. In this paper, we propose AnyScene, a unified occupancy-centric framework for driving scene generation. AnyScene generates semantic occupancy sequences from BEV layouts through a Spatial-Temporal Occupancy Diffusion Transformer that jointly tokenizes BEV and occupancy features in an autoregressive manner. This design enables precise controllability from cross-dataset and user-defined BEV inputs while naturally supporting long-horizon generation. Building upon the generated occupancy, a Geometry-Grounded View Expansion module treats occupancy as the canonical spatial representation and synthesizes temporally consistent multi-view driving videos in a reference-free and autoregressive fashion, supporting flexible camera configurations at inference time. Extensive experiments demonstrate that AnyScene achieves state-of-the-art performance in both occupancy and video generation. It exhibits strong generalization to unseen and customized layouts, and provides measurable benefits for downstream tasks such as sparse-view 3D reconstruction.

</details>


<div id='math.NA'></div>

# math.NA [[Back]](#toc)

### [151] [Improving the Accuracy of the Exponentially Fitted Scheme on Piecewise Uniform Meshes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24336)
*Relja Vulanović*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对一维奇异摄动对流扩散问题，将解分解为简化解u0和边界层分量w，对w采用指数拟合的ASI格式在Shishkin网格上求解。理论证明该方法具有高精度，误差随离散参数增大而减小，在某些情况下甚至随摄动参数减小而减小，验证了ASI格式优于同类Samarskii型格式。当u0为线性时无需分解即可获得更高精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对先前数值结果进行理论确认，证明ASI格式在求解奇异摄动对流扩散问题时优于同类的Samarskii型格式。

Method: 将解分解为u0（精确或近似已知的简化解）和w（边界层分量）；对w采用指数拟合的Allen-Southwell-Il'in (ASI)格式在Shishkin网格及其渐近版本上离散求解。

Result: 所提数值方法具有高精度，误差随离散参数增大而减小，特定情况下随摄动参数减小而减小；当u0为线性时无需分解即可获得更高精度。

Conclusion: ASI格式优于Samarskii型格式，理论结果与先前数值实验一致，新数值实验进一步验证了理论分析。

Abstract: A linear one-dimensional singularly perturbed convection-diffusion problem is solved numerically after its solution is decomposed as $u_0+w$, where $u_0$, the corresponding reduced solution, is treated as a function known exactly or approximately. The component $w$ is then calculated using the exponentially fitted Allen-Southwell-Il'in (ASI) scheme on the Shishkin mesh and its asymptotic version. We prove that this numerical method is highly accurate, with errors that diminish when the discretization parameter increases, and, in some cases, even when the perturbation parameter decreases. This is a theoretical confirmation of earlier numerical results showing that the ASI scheme outperforms the general class of Samarskii-type schemes to which it belongs. Even higher accuracy is proved when $u_0$ is linear, in which case, the decomposition is not needed. New numerical experiments are provided to illustrate all this.

</details>


### [152] [Spectral analysis and sine transform based preconditioning for a structure preserving stabilized scheme approximating the space-fractional Allen Cahn equation with logarithmic potential](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24387)
*Danyal Ahmad,Stefano Serra-Capizzano,Muhammad Sohaib,Cristina Tablino-Possio*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对具有对数Flory-Huggins势的空间分数阶Allen-Cahn方程的初边值问题，采用加权移位Grünwald差分格式逼近分数阶导数，重点研究相关矩阵及矩阵序列的谱特征，并基于此设计预条件子，最后进行数值验证并列出若干开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 求解空间分数阶Allen-Cahn方程时，数值离散产生的矩阵系统条件数较差，影响迭代求解效率，因此需要研究其谱特征并设计高效预条件子以加速收敛。

Method: 采用一阶加权移位Grünwald差分公式离散左右分数阶导数，分析离散所得矩阵及矩阵序列的谱性质，基于谱信息设计预条件子，并对预处理后的矩阵序列进行计算与谱分析。

Result: 通过数值实验验证了所提预条件子的有效性，并给出了若干尚未解决的开放问题。

Conclusion: 本文建立的谱分析与预条件子设计方法能有效处理空间分数阶Allen-Cahn方程数值求解中的矩阵病态问题，为后续研究提供了理论基础与改进方向。

Abstract: We consider an initial boundary value problem of the space fractional Allen-Cahn equation with logarithmic Flory-Huggins potential. As an approximation technique, first-order weighted and shifted Grunwald difference formulae of the left and right fractional derivatives are used. The main focus of the present work is to study the spectral features of the underlying matrices and matrix sequences and to design proper preconditioners based on the spectral information. Then a computational and spectral analysis of the resulting preconditioned matrix sequences is performed. Numerical evidence and a short list of open problems complete the current study.

</details>


### [153] [Incremental SVD for Large-Scale Dynamic Matrices: Accuracy, Subspace Stability, Refresh Strategies, and Financial Factor-Based Risk Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24514)
*Stilyan Staykov*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对高频率场景下数据矩阵逐点更新的问题，提出了一种基于Brand风格增量SVD的实用方案，通过显式投影规则处理秩1条目更新，并将刷新调度作为首要设计维度，在保持低秩分解精度的同时大幅降低计算成本，适用于协方差矩阵与资产因子模型等金融场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在金融等高频场景中，数据矩阵（如收益面板、协方差矩阵）逐时刻更新，下游模型需要每个时间点上的最新低秩分解，但完整SVD计算代价过高，现有增量方法在奇异向量、奇异值保留或长期稳定性上各有不足，亟需一种高效且稳定的增量SVD方案。

Method: 构建统一的增量SVD引擎，同时支持行追加、列追加和秩1条目更新。核心贡献包括：(1) 对秩1条目更新提出显式投影规则 $U'Σ'(V')^\top = P_U(\widehat{A} + δ\,e_ie_j^\top)P_V$，将Brand的秩抑制启发式转化为可量化操作，丢弃子空间外残差并保持秩不变；(2) 将刷新调度作为一等设计维度，系统比较周期性、误差阈值、角度阈值和自适应秩等策略在精度-延迟前沿上的表现；(3) 通过统一框架追踪误差比率、主角度、解释方差和每次更新的运行时间。

Result: 在长序列合成数据和多资产ETF因子模型（用于协方差和投资组合风险估计）上，使用合理的秩和刷新周期，增量SVD的精度与完整SVD相差仅在几个百分点以内，而计算成本大幅降低，能够扩展到批处理SVD不可行的高频场景。

Conclusion: 本文证明了通过合理设计增量更新规则和刷新调度策略，增量SVD能够在高频数据流场景下以极小的精度损失实现接近完整SVD的分解质量，为实时低秩分解提供了一套实用、可量化的工程方案。

Abstract: Return panels, covariances, and large feature matrices evolve one observation or one entry at a time, yet downstream models require an up-to-date low-rank factorization $A_t \approx U_t Σ_t V_t^\top$ on every tick -- a regime where full SVD is prohibitive and existing alternatives sacrifice either singular vectors, singular values, or long-horizon stability. We present a practical, metric-driven study of Brand-style incremental SVD, built around a unified engine that handles row appends, column appends, rank-1 entry updates, and metrics tracking within a single framework, with two core contributions. For rank-1 entry updates, we derive an explicit projection-based rule $U'Σ'(V')^\top = P_U(\widehat{A} + δ\,e_ie_j^\top)P_V$ that keeps rank fixed while discarding only the out-of-subspace remainder in a quantifiable way, turning Brand's rank-suppression heuristic into an operational scheme. We then treat refresh scheduling as a first-class design axis, systematically comparing periodic, error-threshold, angle-threshold, and adaptive-rank policies on the accuracy-latency frontier. A unified framework tracks error ratios, principal angles, explained variance, and per-update runtime on long synthetic streams and a multi-asset ETF factor model for covariance and portfolio-risk estimation. With a sensible rank and refresh cadence, incremental SVD matches full-SVD accuracy within a few percent at a fraction of the cost, scaling to high-frequency regimes where batch SVDs are infeasible.

</details>


### [154] [Optimized multilevel Monte Carlo methods in Banach spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24620)
*Kristin Kirchner,Fabio Nobile,Christoph Schwab,Tommaso Vanzan*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文对Banach空间值随机变量的矩估计进行蒙特卡洛方法理论分析，提出了考虑Rademacher类型常数维度依赖性的改进误差分析，获得了单层和多层蒙特卡洛方法在均值和任意阶单射矩估计上的新颖复杂度结果。针对L^p(S)值随机变量，证明蒙特卡洛估计的L^q收敛率仅由min{q,2}决定，与L^p(S)的Rademacher类型min{p,2}无关，并进一步分析了在更高正则性条件下的最优误差-工作量权衡。数值实验验证了分析结果的sharp性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于Banach空间值随机变量的蒙特卡洛方法误差分析未充分考虑有限维逼近子空间维度对Rademacher类型常数的影响，导致复杂度估计不够精确。本文旨在建立更精细的误差分析框架，揭示维度依赖关系，并探索在L^p空间情形下收敛率与空间几何性质的解耦关系。

Method: 采用理论分析与数值实验相结合的方法。理论上，建立考虑Rademacher类型常数对E_ℓ维度依赖的精细化误差分析，推导单层和多层蒙特卡洛方法在均值和任意阶单射矩估计上的复杂度界限；针对L^p(S)值随机变量，利用其具有的特定逼近性质和双范数正则性假设（X∈L^q(Ω;L^p(S))∩L^p(S;L^q(Ω))），分析L^q收敛率；进一步考虑更高正则性（X∈L^q̃(Ω;L^p(S))∩L^p(S;L^q̃(Ω))）情形下采样误差与强逼近误差的交互作用。数值实验验证理论分析。

Result: 得到了比Kirchner和Schwab (2024)更sharp的复杂度结果；在有利情形下误差-工作量界限与Banach空间的Rademacher类型无关；对于L^p(S)值随机变量，蒙特卡洛估计的L^q收敛率仅由min{q,2}决定，独立于p；在更高正则性条件下，揭示了采样误差与逼近误差的交互机制，获得了优化的误差-工作量界限。

Conclusion: 本文发展了考虑维度依赖性的Banach空间值随机变量蒙特卡洛方法精细化误差分析，揭示了在L^p空间场景下收敛率与空间几何性质(由p刻画)的解耦现象，为高效计算复杂函数空间中的统计量提供了理论依据和分析框架。

Abstract: We present a theoretical and numerical analysis of Monte Carlo methods for the estimation of statistical moments of random variables $X:Ω\rightarrow E$ taking values in a Banach space $E$. For practical computation, we consider finite-dimensional approximation subspaces ${(E_\ell)_{\ell\in\mathbb{N}}\subset E}$ of increasing dimension. We develop a refined error analysis that explicitly accounts for a dependence of the Rademacher type constants on the dimension of $E_\ell$, leading to novel complexity results for single- and multilevel Monte Carlo methods to estimate the mean and injective moments of arbitrary order, which are, in certain cases, sharper than those derived in [Kirchner, Schwab, J. Funct. Anal, 2024]. Moreover, we show that, in favorable cases, the resulting error-vs.-work bounds are independent of the Rademacher type of $E$.
  We then focus on $L^p(S)$-valued random variables for a $σ$-finite measure space satisfying certain approximation properties, and prove that for a random variable $X\in L^q(Ω;L^p(S))\cap L^p(S;L^q(Ω))$, with $q\in (1,\infty)$ and $p\in [1,\infty)$, the $L^q$-convergence rate of a Monte Carlo estimator is determined exclusively by the integrability parameter $\min\{q,2\}$, with no dependence on the Rademacher type $\min\{p,2\}$ of $L^p(S)$. We further investigate the impact of measuring the (multilevel) Monte Carlo error in the $L^q(Ω;L^p(S))$-norm while $X$ possesses additional regularity, $X\in L^{\tilde{q}}(Ω;L^p(S))\cap L^p(S;L^{\tilde{q}}(Ω))$ with $\tilde{q}\in [q,\infty)$. This analysis reveals an interplay between the sampling error and the strong approximation error, and leads to optimized error-vs.-work bounds for both single- and multilevel Monte Carlo methods.
  Numerical experiments confirm the sharpness of the analyses presented.

</details>


### [155] [WINO: A Weak-Form Physics Informed Neural Operator for Hyperelasticity on Variable Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24651)
*Bokai Zhu,Qinghui Zhang,Timon Rabczuk*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出了一种弱形式物理信息神经算子（WINO），结合神经算子的效率与φ-有限元法的几何灵活性，无需配对数据集即可求解偏微分方程，精度高（误差<0.04）且计算时间减少50-80%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经算子方法通常需要大量配对数据或受限于结构化网格，难以灵活处理几何变化；传统有限元方法处理几何变化需要不断重新生成体适形网格，计算成本高。WINO旨在结合神经算子的高效性与φ-FEM的几何灵活性，实现无数据驱动的高效求解。

Method: WINO采用φ-FEM的无拟合方法，用水平集函数φ表示几何；Dirichlet问题通过φ-FEM提升仅学习齐次位移贡献，Neumann问题额外预测辅助场；通过最小化与φ-FEM对齐的弱形式残差平方和以及切割单元辅助方程的平方惩罚项来训练参数；训练后WINO输出可作为神经算子暖启动（NOWS）初始化非线性φ-FEM求解器。

Result: 所有基准测试中WINO误差低于0.04；与传统冷启动求解器相比，计算时间减少50-80%。

Conclusion: WINO作为一种无数据框架，有效结合了神经算子的效率和φ-FEM的几何灵活性，在保持高精度的同时大幅降低计算成本，且训练后的输出可作为暖启动进一步提升求解效率。

Abstract: We propose a Weak-form Physics-Informed Neural Operator (WINO), a data-free framework that combines the efficiency of neural operators with the geometric flexibility of the $\varphi$-finite element method ($\varphi$-FEM). $\varphi$-FEM is an unfitted method that accommodates geometric variations without body-fitted meshes, where the domain geometry is represented by the level-set function $\varphi$. To impose the boundary conditions, Dirichlet problems adopt the $\varphi$-FEM lifting so only the homogeneous displacement contribution is learned, whereas traction-driven Neumann problems additionally predict the auxiliary fields necessary for the unfitted weak formulation. Parameters are trained by minimizing squared weak-form residuals aligned with $\varphi$-FEM together with squared penalties on the cut-cell auxiliary equations, which removes the need for large paired datasets of converged reference solutions. After training, WINO outputs can seed the nonlinear $\varphi$-FEM solvers as neural operator warm starts (NOWS), which reduce iteration counts relative to traditional cold-started solvers. Numerical benchmarks show that WINO achieves high accuracy below 0.04 across all benchmarks, while reducing total computational time by 50--80\% compared with purely data-driven methods.

</details>


### [156] [Sharp Convergence Rates and Optimal Weights for Cimmino's Reflection Algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24692)
*Hemant Sharma*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文从谱理论角度重新分析了Cimmino反射算法求解非奇异线性系统$A\bx=\bb$的收敛性。推导了误差收缩因子$\sprad(M_w)$的精确表达式，证明了对于$n=2$情形，标准单位权重$w_1^*=w_2^*=1$在所有正权重对中全局最优，最小收缩因子$\sprad^*=|\cosθ|$仅由超平面法线间夹角$\theta$决定。还给出了$θ=π/2$时的单步收敛准则及一般$n$维情形的精确谱速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从谱理论视角严格分析Cimmino反射算法的收敛行为，推导误差收缩因子的精确表达式，并研究权重选择的全局最优性，以揭示算法收敛速度与几何参数之间的内在联系。

Method: 将加权迭代重新表述为$\e^{(ν+1)}=M_w\,\e^{(ν)}$形式，其中$M_w = I - A^\top D_w A$；利用谱半径$\sprad(M_w)$刻画误差收缩；针对$n=2$情形推导闭式表达式；通过谱分析证明单位权重的全局最优性。

Result: 获得了$n=2$时收缩因子的闭式表达式$\sprad(M_w) = |1-μ| + \tfrac{1}{2}\sqrt{(w_1-w_2)^2 + 4w_1w_2\cos^2\!θ}$；证明了单位权重$w_1^*=w_2^*=1$全局最优，最小收缩因子为$\sprad^*=|\cosθ|$；给出了正交超平面($θ=π/2$)的单步收敛条件；推广到一般$n$维情形的精确谱速率。

Conclusion: Cimmino反射算法的收敛完全由超平面法线间夹角$\theta$决定，该几何参数同时控制收敛速度和最优权重选择；单位权重是普适最优解。

Abstract: In this paper, Cimmino's classical reflection algorithm for solving the $n\times n$ nonsingular linear system $A\bx=\bb$ is analysed through the lens of spectral theory. Reformulating the weighted iteration as $\e^{(ν+1)}=M_w\,\e^{(ν)}$, where $M_w = I - A^\top D_w A$, the error is shown to contract by the spectral radius $\sprad(M_w)$ at every step, with a sharp, asymptotically tight bound. For $n=2$, a closed-form expression for the contraction factor is derived, \[
  \sprad(M_w) \;=\; |1-μ|
  + \tfrac{1}{2}\sqrt{(w_1-w_2)^2 + 4w_1w_2\cos^2\!θ}, \] where $μ=(w_1+w_2)/2$ and $θ$ denotes the angle between the hyperplane normals. A central result of this paper is that the standard unit weights $w_1^*=w_2^*=1$ are \emph{globally optimal} over all positive weight pairs, uniquely achieving the minimum contraction factor $\sprad^*=|\cosθ|$ -- a quantity determined solely by the geometry of the hyperplane normals. The inter-normal angle $θ$ thus emerges as the single diagnostic parameter governing both convergence speed and weight selection. Extensions to a single-step convergence criterion at $θ=π/2$ and to an exact spectral rate for general~$n$ are also established.

</details>


### [157] [Efficient Numerical Schemes for a Two-Phase Hydrodynamical Model of Active Liquid Crystals and Solids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24758)
*Xuelong Gu,Guanghua Ji,Qi Wang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对主动向列液晶与各向同性固体基底耦合的新型Q张量双相流体动力学模型，提出了几种线性、完全解耦的一阶和二阶时间精度数值格式，证明了离散层面的热力学一致性，并通过数值模拟分析了活性及模型参数对流体-固体相互作用的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主动向列液晶模型缺乏对与固体基底耦合的高效、热力学一致的数值方法，特别是需要设计线性、解耦且满足能量稳定性的格式来模拟主动液晶与固体边界的复杂相互作用。

Method: 基于广义Onsager原理推导模型，提出SGE-BDF1、SGE-PDG（一阶）和SGE-BDF2（二阶）等线性完全解耦格式，通过 stabilization 参数保证稳定性，并在离散层面证明热力学一致性。

Result: SGE-BDF1和SGE-PDG在被动极限下无条件能量稳定；SGE-BDF2在标准有界性假设和足够大稳定参数下关于修正能量稳定。数值模拟揭示了活性参数对液晶-固体相互作用的调控机制。

Conclusion: 提出的解耦线性格式为模拟受限主动向列液晶动力学提供了可靠工具，加深了对软约束下主动液晶物理机制的理解。

Abstract: We propose several linear, fully decoupled numerical schemes with first- and second-order temporal accuracy for a novel Q-tensor-based two-phase hydrodynamic model describing the coupling of active nematic liquid crystal solutions with isotropic solid substrates. The model is derived from the generalized Onsager principle and includes nontrivial terms that contribute zero to the total free-energy dissipation. We prove that the proposed decoupled linear schemes are thermodynamically consistent at the discrete level. In the passive limit, the SGE-BDF1 and SGE-PDG schemes are unconditionally energy stable, while the SGE-BDF2 scheme is energy stable with respect to a modified energy under a standard boundedness assumption and a sufficiently large stabilization parameter. We perform extensive numerical simulations to investigate how activity and other model parameters affect active nematic fluid-solid interactions. Finally, we analyze the physical mechanisms underlying the observed behaviors, providing deeper insight into the dynamics of soft confined active nematic fluids.

</details>


### [158] [String kernel representations in elastostatics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24827)
*Jeremy G. Hoskins,Alan E. Lindsay,Manas Rachh*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的边界积分方程公式，用于求解二维和三维弹性静力学牵引边界值问题。该方法引入了基于Boussinesq-Cerruti半空间解族修改的新型层势（称为弦核），证明了所得积分方程为第二类积分方程，并在不可压缩极限下表现良好，通过数值算例展示了方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统边界积分方程方法在处理弹性静力学牵引边界值问题时存在局限性，尤其是在不可压缩极限下数值性能会退化。因此需要开发一种在不可压缩极限下仍然保持良好性质的边界积分方程格式。

Method: 引入基于Boussinesq-Cerruti半空间解族修改的新型层势（弦核），构建新的边界积分方程公式，从数学上证明其为第二类积分方程。

Result: 新公式在二维和三维弹性静力学牵引边界值问题中均有效，且在不可压缩极限下表现良好，数值算例验证了方法的性能和准确性。

Conclusion: 所提出的弦核边界积分方程方法是一种有效的弹性静力学求解方案，特别适用于不可压缩极限场景，具有理论上的良态性和数值上的稳定性。

Abstract: In this paper we present a new boundary integral equation formulation for the solution of the elastostatic traction boundary value problem in two and three dimensions. The approach relies on the introduction of new layer potentials, called string kernels, which are based on modifications of the Boussinesq-Cerruti family of half-space solutions. We prove that the resulting integral equations are second-kind integral equations, and show that they are well-behaved in the incompressible limit. We illustrate the performance of the method with several numerical examples.

</details>


### [159] [A Hybrid Discontinuous Galerkin Method with Isogeometric-Planewaves Coupling for Periodic Full-Potential Electronic Structure Calculations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24829)
*Baowei Lai,Xucheng Meng,Xiaoxu Li*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合原子贴片中张量积B样条与间隙区平面波的等几何-平面波（IGA-PW）方法，用于周期体系的电子结构计算，通过对称内罚不连续伽辽金公式耦合两个近似空间，并开发了快速傅里叶变换结合切比雪夫校正策略以及迹块DG预处理器，建立了线性特征值问题的先验误差估计，数值实验验证了方法的精度与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 周期体系电子结构计算中，原子核处的库仑奇点导致轨道在核附近具有尖点行为而在间隙区光滑，这种多尺度正则性需要一种混合离散框架来高效处理不同区域的数值特性。

Method: 1) 在原子贴片中采用张量积B样条离散化；2) 通过对称内罚不连续伽辽金（SIPG）公式耦合原子贴片与间隙区的平面波近似空间；3) 开发FFT结合切比雪夫校正策略高效计算受限平面波积分；4) 构建迹块DG预处理器缓解SIPG罚项导致的病态问题并加速迭代特征求解器。

Result: 建立了线性特征值问题的先验误差估计，证明在原子核附近具有代数收敛性，在间隙区具有超代数收敛性。数值实验验证了方法的精度和效率。

Conclusion: IGA-PW方法通过混合B样条与平面波的离散框架，有效处理了全势周期体系电子结构计算中的多尺度正则性问题，在保证精度的同时具有良好计算效率。

Abstract: Full-potential electronic structure calculations for periodic systems retain the Coulomb singularity at the nuclei, which induces cusp behavior of the orbitals near the nuclei while leaving the interstitial region smooth. This multiscale regularity motivates a hybrid discretization framework that combines localized real-space discretizations in atomic patches with plane waves in the interstitial region. In this work, we employ tensor-product B-splines in the atomic patches and couple the two approximation spaces through a symmetric interior penalty discontinuous Galerkin formulation, yielding the isogeometric-plane wave (IGA-PW) method. To efficiently evaluate the restricted plane wave integrals, we develop a combined fast Fourier transform and Chebyshev correction strategy. We also construct a trace-block DG preconditioner to alleviate the conditioning deterioration caused by the SIPG penalty terms and accelerate the iterative eigensolver. In addition, we establish {\it a priori} error estimates for the corresponding linear eigenvalue problem, showing algebraic convergence near the nuclei and superalgebraic convergence in the interstitial region. Numerical experiments demonstrate the accuracy and efficiency of the proposed method.

</details>


### [160] [Shifted Matrix-Sign Reflectors for Prescribed-Index Eigenspace Reflection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24840)
*Jin Zhao*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究了对称矩阵的指定索引反射器 I-2P_k，证明了其与移位矩阵符号函数 sign(H-sI) 的等价性，分析了奇数保号谱滤波器的误差传播与稳定性，提出了移位谱间隙作为控制有限步反射器精度的关键量，并给出了中点移位作为自然默认的理论依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光谱投影仪及其派生的反射器是数值线性代数中的基本对象，但移位反射器在不同移位下的误差行为缺乏系统分析，需要建立理论框架来理解有限步符号滤波器的精度控制与稳定性。

Method: 通过解析奇数保号谱滤波器，证明局部继承性与离散稳定性，推导间隙依赖的 Newton-Schulz 算子界，给出确定性可接受性证书，并通过数值实验分离矩阵函数问题与外层鞍搜索动力学。

Result: 确定了移位谱间隙作为控制有限步反射器精度的关键量，解释了中点移位作为自然默认的原因；数值实验验证了间隙预测，区分了移位符号与原始符号的性能差异，识别了全矩阵符号滤波器的适用与强刚度需要更强符号引擎的区间。

Conclusion: 移位反射器的精度由移位谱间隙决定，中点移位是最优默认选择；有限步符号滤波器具有局部继承性与离散稳定性，可用于实际计算；不同问题区间需要不同强度的符号计算引擎。

Abstract: Spectral projectors and the reflectors derived from them are basic objects in numerical linear algebra. This paper studies the prescribed-index reflector I-2P_k, where P_k is the spectral projector associated with the first k eigenvectors of a symmetric matrix. If a shift s lies in the target spectral gap, then this reflector is exactly the shifted matrix sign sign(H-sI). The exact identity is elementary, but its algorithmic consequences are not: all admissible shifts give the same exact reflector, while finite-step sign filters can have very different errors. We analyze odd sign-preserving spectral filters, prove local inheritance and discrete stability for the induced reflector iterations, derive a gap-dependent Newton-Schulz operator bound, and give deterministic admissibility certificates for inexact and reused shifts. The analysis identifies the shifted spectral margin as the quantity controlling finite-step reflector accuracy and explains why the midpoint shift is the natural default. Numerical experiments separate the matrix-function issues from the outer saddle-search dynamics: controlled spectra verify the margin predictions, low-dimensional tests distinguish shifted signs from raw signs, target-index scans probe non-small k, and Allen-Cahn and dense timing tests identify the regimes in which full-matrix sign filters are useful and the stiff regimes in which stronger sign engines are needed.

</details>


### [161] [Simulation of Gaussian random fields on surfaces using the isogeometric finite element method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24882)
*Helmut Harbrecht,Florian Sonderegger,Remo von Rickenbach*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于等几何有限元法和Balakrishnan积分表示的快速模拟方法，用于在闭合曲面上生成Matérn类协方差函数的随机场，并通过几何多重网格法高效求解线性系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实现闭合曲面上由Matérn类协方差函数生成的随机场的快速模拟。

Method: 求解带有加性白噪声的分数阶随机偏微分方程，采用曲面上的等几何有限元法结合Balakrishnan积分表示，使用几何多重网格法求解线性系统。

Result: 数值结果验证了该方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的等几何有限元法与几何多重网格法相结合，能够高效模拟闭合曲面上的Matérn随机场。

Abstract: We are concerned with the fast simulation of random fields on closed surfaces in $\mathbb{R}^3$ which are generated by the (Whittle-) Matérn class of covariance functions. To this end, we solve the underlying fractional stochastic partial differential equation with additive white noise by using an isogeometric finite element method on the surface in combination with the Balakrishnan integral representation of the solution. The solution of the underlying linear system of equations is performed by means of a geometric multigrid method that naturally underlies the isogeometric approach. Numerical results are presented to demonstrate the approach.

</details>


### [162] [Convergence analysis of structure-preserving schemes for the multicomponent compressible Euler flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24898)
*Jaya Agnihotri,Philipp Öffner*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文对多组分可压缩欧拉系统的有限体积格式在耗散弱解框架下进行了收敛性分析，证明了数值解收敛到耗散弱解，并在经典解存在时通过相对熵方法证明强收敛，数值实验验证了低阶FV和高阶DG格式的理论结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将Lax等价定理推广到非线性情形，为计算流体动力学中的广义解框架提供收敛性理论基础，确保数值格式在保持物理性质（正性）的同时具有收敛保证。

Method: 采用耗散弱（DW）解框架，结合均匀稳定性界和相容性估计证明收敛性；利用相对熵方法和弱-强唯一性原理证明解向经典解的强收敛。

Result: 数值解在DW解框架下收敛到多组分Euler系统；当经典解存在时，近似解强收敛到经典解；低阶FV和高阶DG格式均验证了理论结果。

Conclusion: 所提出的FV格式在DW解框架下是收敛的，且满足正性保持等物理性质，为非线性问题的数值收敛性分析提供了新路径。

Abstract: We present a convergence analysis of a finite volume (FV) scheme for the multicomponent compressible Euler system in the framework of dissipative weak (DW) solutions. DW solutions were introduced as a generalized solution framework in computational fluid dynamics and have recently gained considerable attention. They extend the well-known Lax Equivalence Theorem to nonlinear settings, meaning that if a numerical scheme is both consistent and stable, it will also converge. The FV scheme under consideration preserves key physical properties of the fluid mixture, in particular, positivity of partial densities, pressure, and temperature. Using uniform stability bounds and consistency estimates, we prove that the numerical solutions converge in the framework of DW solutions of the multicomponent Euler system. Applying the relative entropy method and the weak-strong uniqueness principle, we further show that the approximate solutions converge strongly to the classical solution as long as it exists. Numerical experiments confirm the theoretical results, not only for low-order FV methods but also through extended numerical investigations of a higher-order, structure-preserving discontinuous Galerkin scheme.

</details>


### [163] [Debiasing Random Oblique Projections for Subsampled OLS and Fast CUR in High Dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24955)
*Chengmei Niu,Sachin Garg,Michał Dereziński,Zhenyu Liao*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文发展了一个用于高维随机斜投影的统一非渐近理论，揭示了标准随机采样方案中系统统计偏差的存在，该偏差被经典子空间嵌入分析所忽视，并提出了一个原则性的去偏框架来纠正这一偏差。通过子采样最小二乘和快速CUR分解两个典型应用，展示了理论的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有随机采样分析主要依赖子空间嵌入保证，无法精确刻画由采样诱导的非线性随机斜投影的统计偏差。由于（伪）逆操作的非线性特性，即使底层草图是无偏的，这些随机斜投影也会系统性地引入偏差，从而在下游最小二乘和低秩近似解中产生隐藏偏差。

Method: 提出了一个高维随机斜投影的统一非渐近理论框架，识别了标准随机采样方案中系统统计偏差的存在，并设计了原则性的去偏框架来纠正偏差。通过子采样最小二乘（获得锐利的偏差-方差刻画）和快速CUR分解（开发去偏方法以提高近似精度）两个应用验证理论。

Result: 揭示了广泛使用的采样方案中先前未被认识的统计次优性，识别了去偏能够带来可证明改进的条件；去偏后的CUR分解方法具有更优的近似精度；数值实验验证了理论发现。

Conclusion: 随机采样方案存在系统统计偏差，去偏框架可以有效纠正该偏差并在最小二乘和低秩近似中带来可证明的性能改进。

Abstract: Random sampling is a fundamental tool in modern machine learning and numerical linear algebra for reducing the computational cost of large-scale matrix problems. Existing analyses, however, rely primarily on subspace embedding guarantees, which do not precisely characterize the statistical bias of nonlinear random oblique projections induced by sampling, which arises ubiquitously in subsampled least squares and fast low-rank approximation methods. Because (pseudo)inversion is nonlinear, these random oblique projections can be systematically biased even when the underlying sketch is unbiased, thereby introducing hidden bias into downstream least squares and low-rank approximation solutions.
  In this work, we develop a unified non-asymptotic theory for random oblique projections in high dimensions. We show that standard random sampling schemes generally induce a systematic statistical bias overlooked by classical subspace embedding-style analyses, and we propose a principled debiasing framework to correct it. We illustrate the power of the theory through two canonical applications. For subsampled least squares, we obtain sharp bias--variance characterizations, reveal previously unrecognized statistical suboptimality in widely used sampling schemes, and identify when debiasing yields provable improvements. For fast CUR decomposition, we develop a debiased approach with improved approximation accuracy. Numerical experiments further validate our theoretical findings.

</details>


### [164] [A Guided Tour of Modern Domain Decomposition: From Schwarz Iterations to Robust Preconditioners and HPC Implementations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24982)
*Victorita Dolean,Pierre Jolivet,Frédéric Nataf,Pierre-Henri Tournier*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文全面概述了现代区域分解方法（DDMs），从经典Schwarz交替法到现代可扩展预条件技术，重点阐述了适用于不定和高频问题的鲁棒粗空间构造（如GenEO、DtN方法）及高性能实现，为读者提供了理论与实践并重的领域指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区域分解方法已成为大规模偏微分方程数值求解的核心框架，但面对不定和高频等挑战性问题时，传统方法在可扩展性上存在不足。本文旨在系统梳理过去二十年的关键理论进展和设计原则，为读者提供无需遍历全部文献即可理解和应用现代DDMs的实践导向指南。

Method: 本文采用综述性方法，首先回顾重叠分解、单位划分、加性和限制性Schwarz格式等基本概念及其代数解释，进而阐明它们在Krylov子空间求解器中作为预条件子的角色，并论证粗空间修正对于可扩展性的必要性。重点介绍了GenEO和DtN方法等鲁棒粗空间构造技术以及高性能实现策略。

Result: 系统呈现了现代DDMs的理论框架与实践设计原则，特别归纳了针对不定和高频问题的鲁棒粗空间（GenEO、DtN）的构造方法，以及面向高性能计算的可扩展实现方案。

Conclusion: 现代区域分解方法通过粗空间修正等技术已能有效应对挑战性问题（如不定和高频问题），本文为工程和科学计算领域的研究者提供了兼顾理论深度与实践指导的综合性参考。

Abstract: Domain decomposition methods (DDMs) provide a unifying framework for the scalable numerical solution of partial differential equations. Originating from Schwarz's alternating method, they have evolved into a rich family of algorithms that combine local robustness with global convergence acceleration and natural parallelism. Over the past decades, domain decomposition has played a central role in enabling large-scale simulations in numerous applications. This chapter presents an overview of modern DDMs, with a particular emphasis on scalable preconditioning techniques for challenging problems, including indefinite and high-frequency regimes. We revisit the fundamental concepts - overlapping decompositions, partition of unity, additive and restricted Schwarz formulations - and explain their algebraic interpretations. We then clarify their role as preconditioners in Krylov subspace solvers and discuss the necessity of coarse space corrections for scalability. Beyond a the survey aspect, the chapter distills key theoretical insights and practical design principles that have emerged over the past twenty years. Special attention is given to robust coarse spaces (GenEO, DtN-based approaches) and high-performance implementations. The goal is to provide both a coherent overview of the field and a concise, practice-oriented guide for readers seeking to understand and apply domain decomposition methods without navigating the entire literature.

</details>


### [165] [Randomized conjugate gradient least squares](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25034)
*Yun Zeng,Jian-Feng Cai,Deren Han,Jiaxin Xie*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新颖的随机共轭梯度最小二乘（RCGLS）方法，通过在每一步使用迭代草图技术降低维度与计算成本。基于对经典CGLS方法中下降方向由梯度约束校正问题决定的新视角，用满足方差缩减性质的随机坐标梯度替代原始梯度，直接推导出RCGLS方法。证明了RCGLS在期望上线性收敛，且收敛界优于随机坐标下降法。进一步研究了避免全维向量操作的实现方式（解决稀疏矩阵场景下原始RCGLS的主要瓶颈），并展示了如何将RCGLS应用于岭回归问题，生成轻量级、可并行且加速的方法。数值实验验证了理论结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决大规模最小二乘问题中经典CGLS方法计算成本高的问题，利用随机化技术降低每次迭代的维度与计算开销，同时保持收敛性。

Method: 提出RCGLS方法：基于CGLS中下降方向由梯度约束校正问题决定的新视角，用满足方差缩减性质的随机坐标梯度替代原始梯度，结合迭代草图技术降维；针对稀疏矩阵设计避免全维向量操作的实现；扩展至岭回归问题。

Result: 理论证明RCGLS在期望上线性收敛，且收敛界优于随机坐标下降法；数值实验验证了理论结果和方法有效性。

Conclusion: RCGLS是一种高效、可并行且适用于大规模最小二乘和岭回归问题的随机优化方法，具有更优的收敛保证和实际计算效率。

Abstract: We develop a novel randomized conjugate gradient least squares (RCGLS) method for solving least-squares problems, in which iterative sketching is employed at each step to reduce the dimension and hence the computational cost. In particular, we propose a new perspective on the classical CGLS method, where the next descent direction is determined via a constraint correction problem associated with the gradient. Based on this insight, we replace the gradient with a randomized coordinate gradient that naturally satisfies the variance reduction property, leading directly to the proposed RCGLS method. We prove that RCGLS converges linearly in expectation, with a better convergence bound compared to the randomized coordinate descent method. Furthermore, we investigate an implementation of the method that avoids full-dimensional vector operations, which are the major bottleneck of vanilla RCGLS for sparse matrices and render it impractical. We also show how to apply the RCGLS method to solve the ridge regression problem, yielding a lightweight, parallelizable, and accelerated method for such problems. Numerical experiments are provided to confirm our results.

</details>


### [166] [A quasi-orthogonal method based on the inverse operator for Schr{ö}dinger eigenvalue problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25149)
*Shengyue Wang,Aihui Zhou*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出了一种准正交演化模型，利用逆算子计算薛定谔算子的多个特征对，避免了显式正交化的全局通信开销，并建立了相应的离散数值格式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模量子模拟中，计算薛定谔算子的多个特征对因显式正交化带来的全局通信开销而成为计算瓶颈。

Method: 提出准正交演化模型，利用逆算子构造离散数值格式，将数值近似限制在准Stiefel集合内，无需强制显式正交化，自然吸收正交误差并渐近收敛到精确特征函数。

Result: 格式单调耗散目标能量泛函，建立了离散能量、梯度和特征函数逼近的指数收敛率；无限维分析证明允许的时间步长与空间离散化无关，克服了网格依赖的稳定性约束。

Conclusion: 该方案在避免显式正交化的同时实现了全局加速收敛，数值实验验证了理论结果。

Abstract: Computing many eigenpairs of the Schr{ö}dinger operator presents a computational bottleneck in large-scale quantum simulations due to the global communication overhead of explicit orthogonalization. To address this issue, we propose a quasi-orthogonal evolution model utilizing inverse operators and develop a corresponding discrete numerical scheme. Instead of forcing explicit orthogonalization, the proposed framework confines the numerical approximations within a quasi-Stiefel set, ensuring the iterates maintain full column rank without requiring $\left\langle U, U \right\rangle=I_N$. Moreover, the method naturally absorbs orthogonality errors and asymptotically converges to the exact eigenfunctions, even when initialized with non-orthogonal random data. The scheme guarantees monotonic dissipation of the target energy functional, with exponential convergence rates rigorously established for the discrete energy, gradient, and eigenfunction approximations. Furthermore, infinite-dimensional analysis proves that the admissible time step size is independent of the spatial discretization. This property overcomes the mesh-dependent stability constraints typical of conventional explicit or semi-implicit schemes, permitting larger time increments to accelerate global convergence. Numerical experiments validate the theoretical findings.

</details>


### [167] [Arnoldi-Enhanced Multivariate Hermite Interpolation of Manifold-Valued Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25176)
*Yuxuan Li,Qiang Niu,Wubin Zhou*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合多元Arnoldi过程的稳健切线空间埃尔米特插值（THI）方法，通过构造离散正交多项式基来克服数值不稳定性，在SO(3)和S²上的实验表明该方法在计算效率和精度上均优于基于Kriging的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统THI方法在处理流形值数据的高阶逼近时，多元合流Vandermonde矩阵存在固有的数值不稳定性，限制了方法的实际应用。

Method: 利用G-Arnoldi基的递推关系在切空间上直接构造离散正交多项式基，替代直接求解数值病态的Vandermonde矩阵系统。

Result: 给出了C⁰和C¹误差的收敛率：仅用函数值时C⁰误差为O(√M n^{-m})，无导数数据时C¹误差为O(√M h^{-1} n^{-m})，有导数数据时C¹误差为O(√M n^{-(m-1)})。在SO(3)和S²上的实验验证了方法优于Kriging方法。

Conclusion: Arnoldi增强的THI方法有效解决了高阶插值的数值稳定性问题，揭示了逼近阶数、采样密度、填充距离、维度和目标函数正则性之间的相互制约关系。

Abstract: This paper presents a robust enhancement of the Tangent space Hermite Interpolation (THI) method for manifold-valued data by integrating the multivariate Arnoldi process. To circumvent the inherent numerical instability of multivariate confluent Vandermonde matrices, we use a $G$-Arnoldi-based recurrence to construct a discrete orthogonal polynomial basis directly on the tangent space. The method generates better numerical conditioning for high-order approximations. We analyze the convergence rates for both $C^0$ and $C^1$ errors in the multivariate setting. When only function values are used, the $C^0$ approximation error decays as $\mathcal{O}\left(\sqrt{M} n^{-m}\right)$. For the $C^1$ error without derivative data, the rate becomes $\mathcal{O}\left(\sqrt{M} h^{-1} n^{-m}\right)$, where $h$ is the fill distance of the sampling set. When derivative data are additionally available, the $C^1$ error is $\mathcal{O}\left(\sqrt{M} n^{-(m-1)}\right)$. In all cases, $n$ is the polynomial degree, $m$ denotes the regularity of the target function, and $M$ is the number of sampling points. Importantly, as $n$ increases, the required number of points $M$ must also increase. This reveals the interplay among approximation order, sampling density ($M$), fill distance ($h$), dimension ($d$), and the regularity ($m$) of the target function. Extensive numerical experiments conducted on the special orthogonal group $SO(3)$ and the unit sphere $S^2$ show that the Arnoldi-enhanced THI method outperforms the Kriging-based approaches in terms of both computational efficiency and accuracy.

</details>


### [168] [Computing weak-strong uniqueness of a Mach 2000 astrophysical jet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25282)
*Stephan Simonis,Gauthier Wissocq*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 首次通过概率测度推进的统计解方法，验证了马赫2000极端可压缩射流中个体实现发散但统计测度以0.5速率收敛的弱-强唯一性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 极端马赫数可压缩湍流中多维欧拉方程存在非唯一熵解，传统确定性保正格式无法处理混沌不稳定性带来的多解性问题，需要从统计意义理解解的唯一性。

Method: 将统计解定义为概率测度通过矢量格玻尔兹曼方法算子的推前测度；利用优化CUDA内核计算1000个蒙特卡洛样本，最高在320万网格上模拟马赫2000射流；通过内存映射CPU流后处理经验测度，对比样本wise L1误差发散与1-Wasserstein距离下的测度收敛。

Result: 单个流场实现因混沌剪切层不稳定性在L1范数下发散，但概率测度在1-Wasserstein距离下以经验柯西速率0.5收敛到确定的极限测度。

Conclusion: 首次数值验证了极端可压缩 regime 下统计解稳定性，表明统计解为高马赫数湍流提供合理的弱-强唯一性框架。

Abstract: The simulation of extreme Mach astrophysical flows is traditionally viewed through the lens of deterministic positivity-preserving schemes. However, due to phenomena such as Kelvin--Helmholtz instabilities and shock anomalies, the multi-dimensional Euler equations admit a plethora of non-unique entropy solutions in turbulent regimes. For the first time, we computationally explore the weak-strong uniqueness of a Mach 2000 jet by defining the statistical solution as the pushforward of a probability measure through a vectorial lattice Boltzmann method (VLBM) operator. Utilizing highly optimized CUDA kernels, we compute an ensemble of 1000 Monte Carlo samples across a sequence of unprecedentedly refined spatial grids of up to 3.2 million cells, and subsequently post-process the empirical measures via memory-mapped CPU streaming. We contrast the strong sample-wise $L^1$ error divergence with the convergence of the probability measure in the 1-point Wasserstein distance via empirical Cauchy rates. Our mathematical results demonstrate that while individual flow realizations physically diverge due to chaotic shear-layer instabilities, the macroscopic statistical solution converges to a well-defined limit measure at a rate of 0.5. Conclusively, we provide the first numerical verification of statistical solution stability in the extreme compressible regime.

</details>


### [169] [Atomistic-Continuum Coupling by Homogenization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25286)
*Aagashram Neelakandan,Karsten Albe,Bernhard Eidel*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将原子尺度模拟与连续介质有限元耦合的双尺度框架，通过计算均匀化方法（Hill-Mandel能量等价）实现尺度过渡，使经典原子势能模拟能够应用于传统原子模拟无法企及的微米级结构分析，并在单晶铜中验证了其处理强非线性问题的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典原子模拟虽能高保真地解析晶格失稳、缺陷成核和微结构演化，但其可及的系统尺寸远低于微米级结构分析所需，亟需一种能够将原子尺度信息上推到连续尺度的计算方法。
