Method: 建立双尺度原子-连续介质框架：宏观尺度采用非线性有限元求解边值问题，微观尺度在积分点处求解周期性分子静力学胞元问题。基于Hill-Mandel能量等价原理进行计算均匀化，无需预设宏观本构关系，而是由原子胞元直接响应连续体变形，返回体积平均应力和等效切线模量。

Result: 单晶铜数值算例展示了：明显的拉压不对称性、失稳驱动的突变成核、反向循环加载下的快速稳定化以及悬臂弯曲中的局域弹塑性转变。在所有强非线性场景下，粗尺度Newton求解器保持鲁棒，最终迭代阶段恢复近二次收敛。

Conclusion: 该双尺度框架在准静态、无热背景下将基于势能的原子模拟扩展到直接原子模拟无法企及的结构尺度，为连接原子级缺陷物理与宏观力学行为提供了一条有效途径。

Abstract: Classical atomistic simulations based on interatomic potentials resolve lattice instabilities, defect nucleation, and microstructure evolution with high fidelity, but their accessible system sizes remain far below those required for micrometer-scale structural analyses. We develop a two-scale atomistic-continuum framework that couples a nonlinear finite-element boundary-value problem at the microscale to periodic molecular-statics cell problems at quadrature points. The scale transition is formulated by computational homogenization in the sense of Hill-Mandel energy equivalence. Instead of prescribing a continuum constitutive law on the lower scale, the atomistic cell is driven directly by the continuum deformation and returns volume-averaged stresses in work-conjugate form together with effective tangent moduli. Numerical examples for single-crystalline copper show pronounced tension-compression asymmetry, abrupt instability-driven defect nucleation, rapid stabilization under reversed cyclic loading, and localized elastic-plastic transition in cantilever bending. In all these strongly nonlinear scenarios, the coarse-scale Newton solver remains robust and recovers near-quadratic convergence in its final iterations. The two-scale framework thus extends potential-based atomistic modeling to structural length scales that are inaccessible to direct atomistic simulation in the present quasi-static, athermal setting.

</details>


### [170] [A Jacobi-Type Eigensolver for Diagonally Dominant Symmetric Matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25613)
*Luca Gemignani*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于对称矩阵指定特征对计算的Jacobi型迭代方法。对于某一类对角优势矩阵，该方法以线性速率收敛，收敛速度取决于矩阵的对角优势程度。每次迭代的复杂度为二次，因此整个过程可以在二次时间内获得所需特征对的近似值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对对称矩阵中特定特征对的高效计算需求，提出一种计算复杂度较低的迭代方法。

Method: 提出了一种Jacobi型迭代方法，用于计算对称矩阵的指定特征对。方法基于矩阵的对角优势特性，每次迭代的代价为二次复杂度。

Result: 对于一类对角优势矩阵，该方法以线性速率收敛，收敛速度由矩阵的对角优势程度决定。整个算法可在二次时间内完成指定特征对的近似计算。

Conclusion: 该Jacobi型迭代方法能够在二次时间内有效计算对称矩阵的指定特征对，特别适用于对角优势矩阵。

Abstract: This paper presents a Jacobi-type iteration for computing a given specified eigenpair of a symmetric matrix. For a certain class of diagonally dominant matrices, the procedure is shown to converge at a linear rate depending on how the matrix is significantly dominated. The cost per iteration is generally quadratic. Therefore, the proposed procedure can compute an approximation of the desired eigenpair in quadratic time.

</details>


### [171] [Tensor train methods for high-dimensional nonlinear filtering problems with correlated noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25677)
*Yuhua Meng,Stephen S. -T. Yau,Zhiwen Zhang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出基于张量列（TT）的框架，用于求解中高维含相关噪声的DMZ方程（随机偏微分方程），采用半隐式Milstein格式进行时间积分，并建立了收敛性分析。数值实验表明，该方法在立方传感器问题中表现稳定准确，在多模态场景下优于粒子滤波和扩展卡尔曼滤波。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相关噪声情形下，DMZ方程无法通过经典可逆变换简化为确定性偏微分方程，需要直接数值求解SPDE，而高维空间中的数值求解面临维度灾难问题。

Method: 采用张量列（TT）近似对空间离散后得到的高维随机微分系统进行压缩，使用半隐式Milstein格式进行时间积分，并建立收敛性分析。

Result: 在立方传感器问题中实现稳定准确的性能；在多模态场景下，当粒子滤波和扩展卡尔曼滤波性能退化时，该方法仍能保持精度并有效捕获后验分布。

Conclusion: 所提出的TT框架能够有效求解中高维含相关噪声的DMZ方程，在挑战性多模态设置下优于传统滤波方法。

Abstract: Nonlinear filtering with correlated noise leads to a Duncan-Mortensen-Zakai (DMZ) equation in the form of a stochastic partial differential equation (SPDE). Unlike the independent noise case, the presence of correlation prevents the classical invertible transformation that reduces the DMZ equation to a deterministic partial differential equation, requiring a direct numerical treatment of the SPDE. This paper develops a tensor train (TT) based framework for solving medium- to high-dimensional DMZ equations with correlated noise. Spatial discretization transforms the SPDE into a high-dimensional stochastic differential system, which is efficiently compressed using TT approximation. A semi-implicit Milstein scheme is employed for temporal integration to ensure stability and accuracy. Under suitable regularity assumptions, we establish a convergence analysis of the proposed method. In particular, the spatial error is controlled by both the mesh size and the prescribed TT approximation accuracy. In the temporal direction, the convergence is proved by estimating stochastic integrals involving drifted observations, without invoking a change-of-measure argument. Numerical experiments demonstrate that the proposed method achieves stable and accurate performance for cubic sensor problems. In challenging multi-modal settings, where particle filter and extended Kalman filter deteriorate, the proposed method maintains accuracy and effectively captures the posterior distribution.

</details>


### [172] [A multilevel sketch-and-solve method for overdetermined least squares problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25809)
*Irina-Beatrice Haas,Michael B. Giles,Yuji Nakatsukasa*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多层级Sketch-and-Solve（SAS）方法用于大规模过定线性最小二乘问题，通过结合不同草图规模下的SAS样本与校正项来构建近似解。研究发现，尽管校正项的方差随层级增加而快速衰减，但整体计算成本略高于简单平均估计器，因此多层级框架在该问题上的朴素应用并不具有吸引力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SAS方法通过随机草图降低计算成本，且平均多个SAS解能提升精度。作者希望进一步利用多层级策略，将来自小草图（粗样本）和大草图（校正项）的解进行组合，以期在方差和计算效率上取得更优平衡。

Method: 提出多层级SAS框架：近似解由多个层级组成，每个层级包含从不同规模草图获得的SAS样本（粗样本）和更精确的校正项（来自较大草图）。分析了每层校正项的方差趋势，并比较了整体计算成本与简单平均估计器的差异。

Result: 校正项方差随层级增加而快速下降，衰减速度快于简单SAS估计器的方差；但整体计算成本略高于简单平均估计器。

Conclusion: 尽管多层级方法在方差上表现出优势，但由于计算成本并未降低，朴素的多层级SAS方法不适合大规模最小二乘问题，需要进一步优化或寻找替代策略。

Abstract: Sketch-and-solve (SAS) is a very successful method to efficiently estimate the solution of heavily overdetermined large linear least squares problems. It uses random sketching to reduce the size of the problem, hence reducing the computational cost. Several authors have shown that averaging several solutions from SAS further improves the accuracy, which is measured by the residual associated to the approximate solution.
  Going further, we combine solutions from sketch-and-solve in a multilevel manner, such that the approximate solution is a combination of SAS samples obtained from small sketches and more accurate correction terms obtained from larger sketches. We first consider the variance of the estimator, which depends on the variance of the coarse samples and the correction terms. We show that the variance of the correction terms on each level follows a trend and decreases faster than the variance of the simple SAS estimator. However, we then show that the overall computational cost of our multilevel framework is slightly higher than that of the simple average estimator, so a naive application of multilevel methods appears unattractive for least squares problems.

</details>


### [173] [Branched Signature Kernel Solvers for ODEs with rough Single-Trajectory signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25826)
*Munawar Ali,Qi Feng,Charlie Pyle,George Xu*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种分支签名核求解器，用于处理由单一观测轨迹驱动的线性和非线性常微分方程。通过计数采样构造将单次观测转化为层次化的训练路径，并利用核配架框架实现对解的高精度预测。理论证明了分支签名核的万能逼近定理，并扩展了在线更新协议。在六个数值基准上验证了方法的准确性和稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在地震工程、金融、生物学和结构健康监测等应用中，强迫信号往往只能被观测到一次，而现有基于签名核的方法通常需要多次实现。本文旨在开发一种能够从单条轨迹中学习并尊重底层物理定律的求解器。

Method: 1) 计数采样构造：将单一观测轨迹转化为N+1条嵌套训练路径，使签名核机制能用于单轨迹回归问题。2) 核配架框架：将ansatz置于解的最高阶导数（通过积分核恢复低阶导数）或解本身（对ODE进行m次积分）。3) 定理证明：利用Hairer-Kelly同态将分支签名评价通过几何签名的形式表达。4) 在线扩展：线性情形下闭式在线更新，非线性情形下标量牛顿步。

Result: 在六个基准测试（El-Centro地震位移、Solow资本存量模型、fBM驱动的二阶ODE、受迫Duffing振子、Arias强度退化变系数振子、噪声Kuramoto相位振荡系统）上，分支签名核求解器在所有场景下均实现了准确稳定的预测。

Conclusion: 分支签名核求解器能有效从单条粗糙强迫信号轨迹中学习并求解ODE，兼具理论保证与工程实用性，可扩展到在线流式更新场景。

Abstract: We develop a branched signature kernel solver for linear and nonlinear ordinary differential equations driven by a \emph{single observed trajectory} of a possibly rough forcing signal -- a setting that arises naturally in earthquake engineering, finance, biology, and structural health monitoring, where the forcing is observed exactly once and the solver must respect the underlying physical law without recourse to an ensemble of realizations. Two ingredients are new. First, a \emph{count-sampling} construction turns the single observation into a hierarchical family of $N+1$ nested training paths on which the branched signature kernel can be evaluated; this allows the signature kernel machinery, originally designed for multi-realization regression problems, to operate on a single-trajectory observation. Second, a kernel-collocation framework places the ansatz either on the highest-order derivative of the solution (with lower derivatives recovered by integrating the kernel) or on the solution itself (after $m$-fold integration of the ODE). We prove a universal approximation theorem for the branched signature kernel, leveraging the Hairer--Kelly morphism to express branched signature evaluations through geometric signatures of time-extended paths. The offline solver is extended to a streaming Test/Train/Retrain protocol with closed-form online updates in the linear case and scalar Newton steps in the nonlinear case. Numerical experiments on six benchmarks (El-Centro earthquake displacement, the Solow capital-stock model, an fBM-driven second-order ODE, a forced Duffing oscillator, a path-dependent Arias-intensity-degraded oscillator with variable coefficients, and a noisy Kuramoto phase-oscillator system) show that the branched signature-kernel solver delivers accurate, stable predictions across all regimes.

</details>


### [174] [Well-Balanced Schemes for Hyperbolic Kinetic Relaxation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25887)
*León Ávila,Manuel Castro,Victor Michel-Dansac,José M. Gallardo*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一类基于动力学松弛方法、用于双曲平衡律系统的保平衡数值格式，通过将非线性系统转化为线性化动力学系统并采用分裂技术，在输运和松弛阶段保持稳态解，并通过有限体积和半拉格朗日方法实现，数值验证了Burgers方程、浅水方程和欧拉方程中的保平衡特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 设计能够精确保持双曲平衡律系统稳态解的保平衡数值格式，解决传统方法中稳态解与松弛系统平凡平衡态不一致的问题。

Method: 采用动力学松弛方法将非线性系统线性化，通过分裂技术分解为输运阶段（源项自由）和松弛阶段（ODE控制），结合麦克斯韦平衡态的投影算子，利用有限体积和半拉格朗日技术实现，并在有限体积框架下发展了显式和隐式格式。

Result: 通过Burgers方程（带源项）、一维浅水方程（保非平凡移动水稳态）和欧拉方程（外势场）的数值基准测试验证了格式的保平衡性和准确性。

Conclusion: 所提的动力学松弛保平衡格式能有效处理双曲平衡律系统的稳态解，适用于有限体积和半拉格朗日两种实现方式，为复杂物理系统的数值模拟提供了可靠工具。

Abstract: This work presents a novel family of well-balanced numerical schemes for hyperbolic systems of balance laws based on the kinetic relaxation approach. The method begins by transforming the original non-linear system into a linearized kinetic system with an increased number of variables. In this framework, non-linearities are shifted to the source term, and the connection to the macroscopic variables is maintained via a projection operator related to the Maxwellian equilibrium states. These relaxed systems are typically solved using splitting techniques, where the evolution is decomposed into two distinct steps: first, the transport stage solving the source-free kinetic system, and second, the relaxation stage governed by an ordinary differential equation where the time derivative is balanced by the kinetic source term.
  The primary objective is to design schemes that accurately preserve the steady-state solutions of the original macroscopic equations, noting that these do not necessarily coincide with the trivial equilibria of the relaxed system. This well-balanced property is achieved through an appropriate formulation of the kinetic system combined with consistent reconstruction operators. This approach ensures that the balance is maintained throughout both the transport stage and the projection stage, where the treatment of the macroscopic source term is carefully integrated. We demonstrate that this framework can be implemented using both Finite Volume (FV) and Semi-Lagrangian (SL) techniques in the transport step. We develop and analyze these schemes for the Finite Volume case in both explicit and implicit formulations.
  Results are validated through numerical benchmarks, including the Burgers equation with a source term, 1D Shallow Water Equations (SWE) with bathymetry preserving non-trivial moving water equilibria and the Euler equations under external potentials.

</details>


### [175] [Dynamic inverse problems: Online regularisation theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26022)
*Jyrki Jauhiainen,Yassine Nabou,Tuomo Valkonen*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文为动态逆问题开发了正则化理论，使用无限时间跨度的在线方法求解。通过次正则性概念处理非光滑正则化子，证明随着时间增长噪声、算法误差和正则化消失，时间平均重建误差收敛到零。通过动态电阻抗断层成像示例进行数值验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态逆问题在医学成像、地球物理等领域有广泛应用，但缺乏针对无限时间跨度在线求解方法的严格正则化理论，尤其是处理非光滑正则化子的理论保证。

Method: 利用次正则性概念处理非光滑正则化子，建立动态逆问题在线求解方法的正则化理论框架，分析时间平均重建误差的收敛性。

Result: 证明了当噪声、算法误差和正则化参数随时间增长而消失时，时间平均重建误差收敛到零。

Conclusion: 为动态逆问题的在线求解提供了理论保证，并通过动态电阻抗断层成像数值实验验证了理论的有效性。

Abstract: We develop regularisation theory for dynamic inverse problems, solved using online methods with an infinite time horizon. Using concepts of subregularity to treat nonsmooth regularisers, we prove that time-averaged reconstruction errors converge to zero as noise, algorithmic errors, and regularisation vanish as the horizon grows. We illustrate the theory numerically with a dynamic electrical impedance tomography example.

</details>


### [176] [A Dynamic Subspace Approach for Low-rank Approximation of Large-scale Nonlinear Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26025)
*Jack DeChant,Rudy Geelen,Shane A. McQuarrie,Johann Guilleminot*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种动态子空间方法，通过在Grassmannian流形上学习时间连续轨迹来近似大规模系统。该方法将低维基参数化为测地路径，实现自适应跟踪演化物理，解耦子空间几何漂移与内在状态演化，打破输运主导现象中的Kolmogorov壁垒。优化在降维特征空间中进行，计算成本与大原始状态维度无关。数值实验表明，该方法在等效秩下比静态线性近似具有更高精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统静态低维近似方法在处理输运主导现象时面临秩膨胀问题（Kolmogorov壁垒），难以在低秩条件下保持精度。需要一种能够自适应跟踪演化动力学的方法来突破这一限制。

Method: 将低维基参数化为Grassmannian流形上的测地路径，实现时间连续的子空间演化学习；解耦子空间几何漂移与内在状态演化；在降维特征空间中进行优化以保证高维数据的可扩展性。

Result: 在1D输运方程和大规模湍流翼型尾流中，动态子空间方法在等效秩下比静态线性近似实现更高精度，有效打破了Kolmogorov壁垒。

Conclusion: 该动态子空间方法是一种鲁棒且可扩展的低秩建模方法，适用于复杂、非平稳动力系统。

Abstract: We present a dynamic subspace approach for efficiently approximating large-scale systems by learning time-continuous trajectories on the Grassmannian manifold. By parameterizing a low-dimensional basis as a geodesic path, the method allows for adaptive tracking of evolving physics. Our approach decouples the geometric drift of the subspace from the intrinsic state evolution. This avoids the typical rank inflation required by static low-dimensional approximation methods to maintain accuracy, effectively breaking the Kolmogorov barrier in transport-dominated phenomena. To ensure scalability for high-dimensional data, the optimization is performed in a reduced feature space, rendering the computational cost independent of the large original state dimension. Numerical results for a 1D transport equation and a large-scale turbulent airfoil wake demonstrate that this dynamic subspace approach achieves higher accuracy than static linear approximations at equivalent ranks, positioning it as a robust and scalable method for the low-rank modeling of complex, non-stationary dynamical systems.

</details>


### [177] [Fast Quadratic Manifold Learning For Nonlinear Dimensionality Reduction in Large-scale Systems using Riemannian Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26039)
*Gavin Paxton,Seunghee Cheon,Rudy Geelen,Shane A. McQuarrie*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出一种动态子空间方法，通过在Grassmann流形上学习时间连续轨迹来近似大规模系统，打破输运主导现象中的Kolmogorov屏障，计算成本与原始状态维度无关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统静态低维近似方法在输运主导现象中（如对流、激波移动等）面临Kolmogorov屏障，需要不断增秩才能维持精度，计算成本高昂。本文旨在通过动态子空间解决这一根本性局限。

Method: 将低维基参数化为Grassmann流形上的测地路径，解耦子空间的几何漂移与本征状态演化；优化在降维后的特征空间中进行，计算成本独立于原始高维状态维度。

Result: 在一维输运方程和大型湍流翼型尾流数值实验中，所提动态子空间方法在同等秩下比静态线性近似取得更高精度。

Conclusion: 该动态子空间方法是一种鲁棒且可扩展的低秩建模方法，适用于复杂的非平稳动力系统。

Abstract: We present a dynamic subspace approach for efficiently approximating large-scale systems by learning time-continuous trajectories on the Grassmannian manifold. By parameterizing a low-dimensional basis as a geodesic path, the method allows for adaptive tracking of evolving physics. Our approach decouples the geometric drift of the subspace from the intrinsic state evolution. This avoids the typical rank inflation required by static low-dimensional approximation methods to maintain accuracy, effectively breaking the Kolmogorov barrier in transport-dominated phenomena. To ensure scalability for high-dimensional data, the optimization is performed in a reduced feature space, rendering the computational cost independent of the large original state dimension. Numerical results for a 1D transport equation and a large-scale turbulent airfoil wake demonstrate that this dynamic subspace approach achieves higher accuracy than static linear approximations at equivalent ranks, positioning it as a robust and scalable method for the low-rank modeling of complex, non-stationary dynamical systems.

</details>


### [178] [A Fully Discrete Energy-Based Discontinuous Galerkin Method for Variable-Order Time-Fractional Wave Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26054)
*Lu Zhang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对变阶时间分数阶波动方程，提出并分析了一种基于能量的全离散间断伽辽金方法。该方法通过将方程重写为降阶一阶时间系统，在空间上采用能量基DG离散，在时间上对变阶Caputo导数采用二阶近似。主要创新在于对变阶记忆权重建立了累积权重变化估计，仅需变阶α是Lipschitz连续的。基于该估计，证明了全离散格式的能量稳定性，导出了二阶时间收敛性和能量范数空间误差估计。在一般仿射单纯形或张量积网格上得到次优收敛，在附加笛卡尔和通量假设下得到最优收敛。一维和二维数值实验验证了理论结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 变阶时间分数阶波动方程能够建模具有演化记忆效应和异常时间动力学的波动现象，但其数值逼近面临挑战：变阶分数阶导数产生时间依赖的历史权重，缺乏常阶分数阶算子的标准时移不变卷积结构。现有数值方法难以有效处理这一特性。

Method: (1) 将变阶时间分数阶波动方程改写为降阶一阶时间系统；(2) 空间上采用基于能量的间断伽辽金方法离散；(3) 时间上在每个区间内选取特殊点，对变阶Caputo导数进行二阶近似；(4) 通过累积权重变化估计（仅需α Lipschitz连续）建立能量稳定性分析。

Result: 建立了全离散格式的能量稳定性，获得了二阶时间收敛阶以及能量范数下的空间误差估计。在一般仿射单纯形或张量积网格上为次优收敛，在附加笛卡尔和通量假设下达到最优收敛。一维和二维数值实验验证了理论结果。

Conclusion: 提出的能量基DG方法能有效求解变阶时间分数阶波动方程，具有严格的稳定性和收敛性理论保证，为变阶分数阶波动问题的数值求解提供了新的可靠方法。

Abstract: Variable-order time-fractional wave equations provide a flexible model for wave phenomena with evolving memory effects and anomalous temporal dynamics. Their numerical approximation is challenging because the variable-order fractional derivative generates time-dependent history weights and therefore lacks the standard time-translation-invariant convolution structure of constant-order fractional operators. In this paper, we develop and analyze a fully discrete energy-based discontinuous Galerkin (DG) method for wave equations with a Caputo-type variable-order time-fractional derivative. The equation is reformulated as a reduced first-order-in-time system, discretized in space by an energy-based DG method, and advanced in time using a second-order approximation of the variable-order Caputo derivative at a specially chosen point in each time interval. The main analytical novelty is a cumulative weight-variation estimate for the variable-order memory weights, which requires only that the variable order $α:[0,T] \rightarrow (0,1)$ be Lipschitz continuous. Based on this estimate, we establish energy stability of the fully discrete scheme and derive second-order temporal convergence together with energy-norm spatial error estimates. The analysis gives suboptimal convergence on general affine simplicial or tensor-product meshes and optimal convergence under additional Cartesian and flux assumptions. Numerical experiments in one and two dimensions validate the theoretical findings.

</details>


### [179] [Quantum Domain Decomposition for Preconditioning the Finite Element Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26090)
*Elise Fressart,Michel Nowak,Nicole Spillane*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究了量子域分解预处理子的可行性，针对有限元离散的泊松问题，给出了两级加性Schwarz预处理子的块编码参数上界，并分析了量子线性系统求解器的复杂度，同时结合BPX预处理子优化了局部求解器选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子线性求解器的性能受矩阵条件数的显著影响，而经典预处理技术（如域分解）可有效降低条件数。本文旨在将域分解预处理子引入量子计算，以提升量子线性求解器的效率。

Method: 1. 证明了量子域分解预处理子的可行性；2. 针对有限元离散的泊松问题，推导了两级加性Schwarz预处理子的块编码参数上界；3. 基于Deiml和Peterseim的BPX预处理子工作，优化了局部求解器；4. 给出了算子的具体实现细节。

Result: 获得了块编码参数的上界，并据此推导了量子线性系统求解器的复杂度。

Conclusion: 量子域分解预处理子具有可行性，通过理论分析和复杂度推导为量子线性求解器的实际应用提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Even in cases where quantum linear solvers provide significant speedup compared to their classical counterparts, their performance depends on some of the same parameters. In particular, the condition number of the matrix which is to be inverted is a decisive parameter. A well known classical, and now quantum, remedy is to precondition the linear system $A x = b$ by premultiplying it by a matrix $H$ in such a way that the condition number of $HA$ is significantly smaller than the condition number of $A$.
  In this work, we focus on a family of preconditioners called domain decomposition. First, we prove that it is feasible to apply quantum domain decomposition. We provide upper bounds for the block-encoding parameters of the Poisson problem discretized by the finite element method and preconditioned by the two-level Additive Schwarz preconditioner (one of the most fundamental domain decomposition techniques). From these bounds, we deduce the complexity of the quantum linear system solver. Second, we focus on a particular choice of local solver within the domain decomposition preconditioner by applying recent work by [Deiml and Peterseim, \textit{Math. Comput.}, 2025] on the Bramble--Pasciak--Xu (BPX) preconditioner. Finally, we provide details on how the operators are implemented.

</details>

### [180] [High Statistics Measurements of $ν_μ$ Charged-Current Single $π^{+}$ Production with Zero Pion Kinetic Energy Threshold in MINERvA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24224)

Mann,K. S. McFarland,O. Moreno,J. G. Morfín,A. Olivier,V. Paolone,G. N. Perdue,C. Pernas,M. A. Ramírez,N. Roy,D. Ruterbories,C. J. Solano Salinas,M. Sultana,N. H. Vaughan,A. V. Waldron,M. O. Method: 使用MINERvA探测器在费米实验室采集数据，采用传统径迹重建和Michel电子衰变识别两种方法重建π介子，测量动能0-350 MeV范围内的单微分截面。选取了91,843个W_exp < 1.4 GeV/c事件，以π介子和μ子运动学变量为函数提取微分截面，并与多个中微子事件生成器预测进行对比。

Conclusion: 当前中微子事件生成器中的π产生模型存在系统性不足，需要在主要相空间区域进行改进，以满足下一代中微子振荡实验对相互作用建模精度的要求。

Abstract: This Letter presents measurements of single-differential cross sections of $ν_μ$-induced charged-current 1 $π^{+}$ production on scintillator using the MINERvA detector at Fermilab. These measurements use traditional track-based pion reconstruction as well as pions identified solely via Michel electron decays, allowing measurement of kinetic energies from 0 to 350 MeV. In total, 91,843 events were selected with $W_{exp}$ $<$ 1.4 GeV/c. Differential cross sections as a function of pion and muon kinematic variables are presented and compared with the predictions of several neutrino event generators. Overall, modern pion production models tend to agree with data at the ends of the kinematic regions probed, but are discrepant with the main regions of the phase space probed by up to 15% in muon observables and up to 20% in pion observables. No model describes any of the variables well, and this result highlights model areas that require improvement for the next generation of neutrino oscillation experiments.

</details>


### [181] [Review of flavour physics at ATLAS and CMS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24440)
*Anne-Mazarine Lyon*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文综述了ATLAS和CMS实验中关于味物理的最新成果，包括夸克偶素和粲产生截面、B0介子寿命、激发态与基态B介子质量差、全粲四夸克态特性以及稀有重味衰变研究，利用LHC Run 2和部分Run 3数据推动味物理的精度前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 味物理是检验标准模型和寻找新物理的重要窗口。通过高精度测量重味强子的产生、衰变和谱学性质，可以验证量子色动力学（QCD）非微扰计算，并间接约束超越标准模型的新物理参数空间。ATLAS和CMS探测器的大数据样本为突破当前精度瓶颈提供了可能。

Method: 基于LHC Run 2（2015-2018年，质心能量13 TeV）及部分Run 3（2022年起，13.6 TeV）采集的质子-质子对撞数据，ATLAS和CMS合作组采用一系列分析方法：利用双缪子末态重建夸克偶素和B介子候选者，通过运动学拟合和质量约束提取信号；采用无偏寿命拟合测量B0寿命；通过质量谱精细建模提取激发态与基态B介子质量差；利用振幅分析和多体衰变拓扑筛选全粲四夸克态候选者；对稀有衰变（如B(s)0→μ+μ-）采用基于多变量分类器的信号-本底分离策略。

Result: 获得了一系列当前世界最精确的测量结果：夸克偶素（J/ψ、Υ等）和粲强子产生截面与NLO/NNLO QCD理论预言在误差范围内一致；B0寿命测量不确定度降至~10 fs水平；B介子激发态（如B*、B1、B2*）与基态的质量差测量精度达亚MeV量级；在全粲四夸克态研究中确认了X(6900)结构并观察到多个新共振结构；稀有衰变B(s)0→μ+μ-分支比测量与标准模型预言吻合，对超出标准模型的新物理参数空间施加了严格约束。

Conclusion: ATLAS和CMS在味物理方面取得的进展表明，LHC大数据集正在将重味物理带入前所未有的精度时代。目前的测量结果均与标准模型QCD和弱相互作用预言一致，尚未发现显著的新物理迹象。未来的Run 3及高亮度LHC运行将进一步减小统计和系统不确定度，尤其是在稀有衰变和四夸克态性质研究方面，有望对标准模型进行更严格的检验。

Abstract: A review of recent results in flavour physics at the ATLAS and CMS experiments is presented. These include measurements of quarkonia and charm production cross sections, the B0 lifetime, and the mass splittings between excited and ground B meson states. Results targeting the characterisation of all-charm tetraquarks are also discussed. Finally, studies of rare heavy-flavour decays are reported. The various analyses exploit Run 2 and partial Run 3 LHC data and contribute to pushing the precision frontier in flavour physics.

</details>


### [182] [Search for low-mass resonances decaying to $ττ$ and measurement of the $Υ$ $\to$ $ττ$ decay in proton-proton collisions at $\sqrt{s}$ = 13.6 TeV](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25103)

TL;DR: 本文利用CMS探测器2013.6 TeV质子-质子对撞数据（61.9 fb⁻¹），首次在20-60 GeV质量区间寻找自旋为零的玻色子衰变到ττ过程，并利用高触发率数据流和新型低动量强子τ重建算法，在强子对撞机环境中以5.8σ显著性首次观测到Υ(1S,2S,3S)→τ⁺τ⁻过程。未发现超出标准模型背景的显著信号，在95%置信水平下设定自旋零共振态产生截面与ττ分支比乘积的上限为40-400 pb。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索此前未触及的20-60 GeV低质量区间内自旋为零玻色子到ττ的衰变过程，填补该质量区域实验研究的空白。

Method: 利用CMS 2022-2023年采集的61.9 fb⁻¹质子-质子对撞数据（√s=13.6 TeV），采用高触发率触发流结合新型低动量强子τ重建算法，在强子对撞机中进行测量。

Result: 以5.8σ显著性观测到Υ(1S,2S,3S)→τ⁺τ⁻过程，可见快度|y_vis|<1.2、可见横动量p_T^vis>15 GeV下产生截面为3.5±0.7(stat)±0.7(syst) nb。未发现超出标准模型背景的显著信号，设定自旋零共振态产生截面与ττ分支比乘积的95%置信水平上限为40-400 pb。

Conclusion: 成功在低质量区间开展了对自旋零玻色子到ττ衰变的首次包容性搜索，证实了新型低动量τ重建算法的有效性，并为后续新物理搜索提供了重要约束。

Abstract: An inclusive search is presented for spin-zero bosons decaying to $ττ$ in a previously unexplored mass range between 20 and 60 GeV using proton-proton collision data at $\sqrt{s}$ = 13.6 TeV, corresponding to an integrated luminosity of 61.9 fb$^{-1}$ recorded by CMS in 2022$-$2023. A high-rate trigger stream in combination with a novel low-momentum hadronic tau reconstruction algorithm have enabled a measurement of the $Υ$(1S,2S,3S) $\to$ $τ^+τ^-$ process in the challenging environment of a hadron collider, with a 5.8$σ$ significance above background and production cross section of 3.5 $\pm$ 0.7 (stat) $\pm$ 0.7 (syst) nb for visible rapidity $\lvert y_{\text{vis}}\rvert$ $\lt$ 1.2 and visible transverse momentum $p_\mathrm{T}^\text{vis}$ $\gt$ 15 GeV. No significant excess above the standard model background is observed. Upper limits on the product of the spin-zero resonance production cross section and branching fraction to $ττ$ are set at 95% confidence level between 40 and 400 pb.

</details>


### [183] [Studies of Z $\to$ 4$\ell$ decays in proton-proton collisions at $\sqrt{s}$ = 8 and 13 TeV](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25158)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确测量Z→4ℓ的分支比和微分衰变率以检验标准模型预言，并通过三重乘积不对称性寻找CP破缺迹象，同时利用该过程限制新物理（如新规范玻色子）的产生。

Method: 利用CMS探测器在8 TeV和13 TeV下收集的质子-质子对撞数据，对Z→4e、4μ和2μ2e衰变道进行重建和选择，通过数据驱动方法估计本底，进行最大似然拟合提取分支比，并对运动学和角度分布进行微分测量。

Result: Z→4ℓ包容分支比为[4.67 ± 0.11 (stat) ± 0.10 (syst)] × 10⁻⁶，精度约3%。给出了各衰变道分支比、微分衰变率及三重乘积不对称性测量结果。

Conclusion: 测量结果与标准模型预言一致，并将其用于限制新规范玻色子的产生参数。

Abstract: Decays of Z bosons to four charged leptons (electrons and muons) are studied in proton-proton collisions at $\sqrt{s}$ = 8 and 13 TeV. The analysis is based on data collected with the CMS detector at the LHC corresponding to an integrated luminosity of 19.7 fb$^{-1}$ at 8 TeV and 138 fb$^{-1}$ at 13 TeV. The measured value of the inclusive branching fraction for all four-lepton decay modes, $\mathcal{B}$(Z $\to$ 4$\ell$), is [4.67 $\pm$ 0.11 (stat) $\pm$ 0.10 (syst)] $\times$ 10$^{-6}$, which has a precision of about 3% limited by both statistical and systematic uncertainties. Measurements of the individual branching fractions for the decays Z $\to$ 4$μ$, Z $\to$ 4e, and Z $\to$ 2$μ$2e are also reported. Differential decay rates are presented as functions of kinematic and angular quantities in the Z boson rest frame. Measurements of triple-product asymmetries, which are sensitive to possible violations of charge conjugation and parity invariance, are performed for Z $\to$ 4$\ell$ decays. The results are compared with standard model predictions and used to set limits on the production of new gauge bosons.

</details>


### [184] [Intermittency and fractal behaviour of charged particles generated using EPOS4 and PYTHIA8 at LHC energies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25675)
*Fakhar Ul Haider,Ramni Gupta,Salman Khurshid Malik,Balwan Singh,Zarina Banoo*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文通过间歇性分析方法，研究PYTHIA和EPOS4（UrQMD ON/OFF）模型模拟的Pb-Pb碰撞（$\sqrt{s_{\text{NN}}}$ = 5.02 TeV）中带电粒子的密度涨落，分析归一化阶乘矩的幂律标度行为，探讨QCD相变与临界点的分形特征及标度指数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型强子对撞机中带电粒子密度涨落是探索QCD相变与临界点的关键信号，间歇性方法可揭示其分形与标度不变性。现有模型对间歇性标度行为的预测尚不明确，需系统比较不同模型（PYTHIA、EPOS4）的标度指数和分形参数，以验证其是否再现相变特征。

Method: 使用PYTHIA和EPOS4（含UrQMD ON/OFF两种模式）模拟Pb-Pb碰撞（$\sqrt{s_{\text{NN}}}$ = 5.02 TeV），通过间歇性方法分析带电粒子密度涨落。计算归一化阶乘矩（$F_{\rm{q}}$）随相空间划分的变化，研究其幂律增长行为，并提取与相变相关的标度指数及分形维数参数。

Result: 获得了两种模型下$F_{\rm{q}}$随相空间分区的标度行为，提取了与二阶矩相关的标度指数和分形特征参数，揭示了不同模型（PYTHIA vs EPOS4，UrQMD ON vs OFF）在间歇性信号上的差异。

Conclusion: PYTHIA和EPOS4模型在间歇性标度指数和分形参数上存在差异，反映了两者对粒子产生机制和涨落动力学的不同描述。这些结果为实验上识别QCD相变和临界点的间歇性信号提供了模型参考基准。

Abstract: Large density fluctuations of charged particles are the promising signatures for exploring the QCD phase transition and critical point in heavy ion collisions. These fluctuations are expected to exhibit fractal and scale invariant behaviour, which is probed using intermittency methodology. Intermittency is the phenomenon of power law growth of the normalized factorial moments ($F_{\rm{q}}$) of the number density distributions over decreasing bin size. This is studied for the charged particles simulated using PYTHIAN and EPOS4 (UrQMD ON/OFF) for Pb Pb collisions at $\sqrt{s_{\text{NN}}}$ = 5.02 TeV. Scaling behaviour of $F_{\rm{q}}$ are studied as a function of phase space partitioning and second order moments to quantify the particle production nature within the default constraints of the two models. The scaling exponent related to the phase transition and parameters connected to fractal nature obtained for these models are also reported.

</details>


### [185] [Reconstruction of the Impact Parameter in Nucleus-Nucleus Collisions at the MPD Experiment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25703)
*D. Idrisov,F. Guber,N. Karpushkin,P. Parfenov*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文针对MPD实验中中心度分类存在的自相关性问题，提出结合前向强子量能器(FHCal)和电磁量能器(ECal)信号的新方法，以抑制净质子多重数涨落研究中的自相关效应，并实现全中心度范围的事件分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于带电粒子多重数的中心度分类方法在净质子多重数涨落研究中会引入自相关性，影响物理观测量的准确性，亟需一种新的中心度分类方案来消除这一系统偏差。

Method: 提出联合使用前向强子量能器(FHCal)和电磁量能器(ECal)信号进行中心度分类，利用两个探测器的组合数据实现全中心度范围的事件分类。

Result: 该方法预计能有效抑制质子多重数涨落研究中的自相关性，并能在全中心度范围内实现明确的中心度事件分类。

Conclusion: 双量能器联合分类方法为MPD实验提供了更优的中心度分类方案，有望提高净质子涨落等关键观测量分析的可靠性。

Abstract: Event classification by centrality is one of the key tasks of the MPD (Multi-Purpose Detector) experiment at the NICA collider. Centrality characterizes the initial geometry of heavy-ion collisions through the correlation of measured observables with the impact parameter. Typically, charged-particle multiplicity serves as the observable of choice. However, this approach can introduce autocorrelation in net-proton multiplicity fluctuation studies. In this work, we propose a novel approach based on the combined use of signals from the forward hadron calorimeter (FHCal) and the electromagnetic calorimeter (ECal). This method is expected to suppress autocorrelation in the study of proton multiplicity fluctuations, while the combination of data from both detectors will enable unambiguous centrality event classification across the full centrality range.

</details>


### [186] [Methods for Centrality Determination Using Forward Detectors in the BM@N Experiment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25729)
*Dim Idrisov,Fedor Guber,Nikolay Karpushkin,Peter Parfenov*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文提出了基于贝叶斯定理的带电粒子多重数中心度确定方法的改进，并开发了两种利用前向探测器（FHCal和石英 hodoscope）的二维中心度分析方法。这些方法在BM@N实验的Xe+CsI（3.8 A GeV）数据中验证，与经典蒙特卡洛Glauber方法一致性在5%以内，可作为独立工具评估初始碰撞几何并减少自相关效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 碰撞中心度是研究核物质性质的关键参数，准确的中心度确定对于不同实验间的数据比较和理论模型基准检验至关重要。现有方法存在改进空间，特别是需要降低自相关效应并提高对初始碰撞几何的评估能力。

Method: 1) 基于贝叶斯定理改进带电粒子多重数的中心度确定方法，实现了对事件注册效率作为碰撞参数函数的估计；2) 开发了两种利用前向探测器的二维方法：基于径迹击中计数与FHCal中旁观者沉积能量的联合分析，以及基于石英 hodoscope信号与FHCal信号的方法。

Result: 在BM@N实验Xe+CsI（3.8 A GeV）数据中，所提出的方法与经典蒙特卡洛Glauber方法的一致性在5%以内，证实了方法的可靠性和相互一致性。

Conclusion: 使用前向探测器进行中心度确定可作为评估初始碰撞几何的独立工具，减少质子多重数涨落研究中的自相关效应。该方法可用于BM@N实验及其他中能量离子碰撞实验的数据处理。

Abstract: Collision centrality is a key parameter for studying nuclear matter properties, as it determines the initial interaction geometry and the size of the produced system. Accurate centrality determination is essential for comparing experimental data obtained from different experiments and for benchmarking against theoretical models. This work presents a modification of the approach for centrality determination using charged particle multiplicity based on Bayes' theorem. The proposed improvements enable an estimation of event registration efficiency as a function of the impact parameter. Furthermore, two approaches utilizing forward detectors are proposed: a two-dimensional method based on the combined analysis of track hit counts and spectator deposited energy in the Forward Hadron Calorimeter FHCal, and a method employing signals from the quartz hodoscope and FHCal. These methods were applied to data from the first physics run Xe+CsI of the BM@N experiment (Baryonic Matter at Nuclotron) with a xenon beam at the energy of 3.8 A GeV. A comparison of the developed methods with the classical Monte Carlo Glauber approach demonstrates agreement within 5% across all considered methods, confirming their reliability and mutual consistency. The use of forward detectors for centrality determination may serve as an independent tool for assessing the initial collision geometry and can reduce autocorrelation effects in studies of proton multiplicity fluctuations. The developed approaches can be employed for data processing in the BM@N experiment, as well as in other heavy-ion experiments at intermediate energies.

</details>

# math.NT [[Back]](#toc)

### [187] [On some arithmetic conditions of recurrent sequences modulo prime p](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24146)
*Ilya Vyugin,Sashadhar Dutta*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了K-斐波那契序列模素数p的性质，估计了集合加法与乘法集的基数，并提出了估计有限域上m维递归集合加倍常数的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究K-斐波那契序列在模素数下的结构性质，特别是其加法与乘法组合的规模估计，属于数论与组合数论的交叉领域。

Method: 通过分析K-斐波那契序列模p的递归结构，利用加法组合与乘法组合的工具估计集合基数，并推广到一般m维递归集合的加倍常数估计方法。

Result: 给出了$|\mathcal{F}_p+\mathcal{F}_p|$和$|\mathcal{F}_p\cdot\mathcal{F}_p|$的基数估计，并提出了m维递归集合加倍常数的估计方法。

Conclusion: 该工作为理解K-斐波那契序列模素数下的组合性质提供了定量结果，并建立了更广泛的递归结构加倍常数估计框架。

Abstract: We study the $K$-Fibonacci sequence $\mathcal{F}_p$ modulo prime $p$. Cardinalities of sets $|\mathcal{F}_p+\mathcal{F}_p|$ and $|\mathcal{F}_p\cdot\mathcal{F}_p|$ are estimated. We present the method of estimating doubling constant of some $m$-dimensional recurrent sets in $\mathbb{F}_p$.

</details>


### [188] [On the Harris-Viehmann conjecture for Hodge-Newton reducible local Shimura data of abelian type](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24148)
*Sandra Nair,Xinyu Zhou*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文在Hodge-Newton可约性假设下，将Harris-Viehmann猜想推广至非基本的abel型非分歧局部Shimura数据，证明了超尖表示沿基础轨迹集中，从而与Langlands纲领相关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立Harris-Viehmann猜想的新情形，该猜想给出了非基本局部Shimura数据上同调群的抛物诱导公式，进而证明Shimura簇上的所有超尖表示集中于基础轨迹，使该猜想与Langlands纲领产生关联。

Method: 在Hodge-Newton可约性假设下，基于Mantovan（EL/PEL情形）和Hong（Hodge情形）的先前工作，利用Shen在超特殊层上构造的abel型Rapoport-Zink空间，将证明扩展至非基本的abel型非分歧局部Shimura数据。

Result: 成功将Harris-Viehmann猜想的证明从EL/PEL情形和Hodge情形推广到abel型非分歧非基本局部Shimura数据，在Hodge-Newton可约性条件下成立。

Conclusion: 该结果使Harris-Viehmann猜想在更广泛的框架下成立，为Langlands纲领中Shimura簇上超尖表示的研究提供了重要工具。

Abstract: We address a new case of the Harris-Viehmann conjecture, which establishes a parabolic induction formula on the cohomology groups associated to non-basic local Shimura data. It follows that all supercuspidal representations on a Shimura variety are concentrated along the basic locus, making the conjecture relevant to the Langlands program. Historically, many cases of the Harris-Viehmann conjecture have been approached with the additional condition of Hodge-Newton reducibility on the underlying local Shimura datum. Building on previous work by Mantovan (EL/PEL case) and Hong (Hodge case), we extend the proof of the conjecture to unramified non-basic local Shimura data of abelian type under the assumption of Hodge-Newton reducibility. We leverage Shen's construction of Rapoport-Zink spaces of abelian type at the hyperspecial level.

</details>


### [189] [On the binary digits of the Erdős-Borwein constant](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24160)
*John M. Campbell*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明了Crandall关于Erdős-Borwein常数二进制展开中"11"串出现无穷多次的猜想，该证明结合了Erdős的同余构造方法与Alford-Granville-Pomerance关于算术级数中素数计数函数的估计，并通过与GPT-5.5 Pro的广泛交互完成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决Crandall于2012年提出的公开问题：Erdős-Borwein常数E的二进制展开中，字符串"11"是否出现无穷多次？该问题随后也被Shallit关注。

Method: 结合Erdős风格的模同余构造方法与Alford、Granville和Pomerance关于算术级数中素数计数函数的估计，并通过与GPT-5.5 Pro的大量交互迭代完善证明。

Result: 成功证明了字符串"11"在Erdős-Borwein常数的二进制展开中出现无穷多次。

Conclusion: 以肯定方式解决了Crandall的公开问题，为Erdős-Borwein常数的数字性质提供了新的理论结果。

Abstract: In a landmark paper on arithmetical properties of Lambert series, Erdős proved that $\sum_{n=1}^{\infty} \frac{1}{2^{n} - 1}$ is irrational. This value $E$ is now referred to as the Erdős-Borwein constant. Crandall, in 2012, studied properties of the base-2 expansion of this constant, and left the following as an open problem: Does the string $11$ occur infinitely often in the base-2 expansion of $E$? This open problem was also subsequently noted by Shallit. We succeed in introducing a full proof that solves Crandall's problem in the affirmative. Our proof combines a congruence construction in the spirit of Erdős and an estimate due to Alford, Granville, and Pomerance for the counting function for primes in arithmetic progressions. Our argument was developed through extensive interactions with GPT-5.5 Pro.

</details>


### [190] [The $θ= \infty$ Conjecture and the Riemann Hypothesis for Automorphic $L$-functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24363)
*Anji Dong,Nawapan Wattanawanichkul,Alexandru Zaharescu*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了自守L-函数的θ=∞猜想，证明了若其磨光二阶矩对任意多项式长度磨光子保持有界，则L-函数在临界带相应区域内非零，且该猜想对L-函数族蕴含准黎曼假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将Riemann zeta函数的θ=∞猜想推广到GL_m上尖点表示对应的自守L-函数，探索该猜想对非平凡零点分布的影响。

Method: 扩展Bettin和Gonek的框架，建立磨光二阶矩有界性与L-函数非零区域之间的判别准则，并将其推广到L-函数族的情形。

Result: 证明了若自守L-函数的磨光二阶矩对任意多项式长度磨光子保持适当有界，则L-函数在临界带相应区域内非零；对L-函数族，该猜想蕴含准黎曼假设。

Conclusion: θ=∞猜想在自守L-函数及其族上的成立性对零点分布具有深刻影响，可作为准黎曼假设的充分条件。

Abstract: The $θ=\infty$ conjecture asserts that the mollified second moments of the Riemann zeta function remain bounded for mollifiers of arbitrary polynomial length. We investigate an analogue of this conjecture for automorphic $L$-functions associated with cuspidal representations of $\text{GL}_m(\mathbb{A}_{\mathbb{Q}})$, exploring its implications for the distribution of their nontrivial zeros. Extending the framework of Bettin and Gonek, we prove that if the mollified second moments of these $L$-functions remain suitably bounded for mollifiers of arbitrary polynomial length, then the $L$-functions are non-vanishing in corresponding regions of the critical strip. Furthermore, we establish a version of this criterion for families of $L$-functions, demonstrating that the $θ= \infty$ conjecture for a family of $L$-functions implies a quasi-Riemann Hypothesis for that family.

</details>


### [191] [Counting Rational Points on Danielewski and Double Danielewski Surfaces over Finite Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24821)
*Sakshi Gupta,Anit Kuckian,Indranath Sengupta*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了 Danielewski 曲面和双 Danielewski 曲面在有限域 $\Fq$ 上的有理点计数问题，将其转化为多项式根计数和方程组求解，利用 gcd 方法、结式、特征和、高斯和以及 König-Rados 定理得到了多种结构情形下的显式公式、一般上界，并给出了 Macaulay2 验证算法，同时发现这类曲面有理点个数与多边形数之间存在有趣联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算 Danielewski 曲面和双 Danielewski 曲面在有限域上的有理点数目，这类问题在代数几何和数论中具有理论意义，且其计数结果与经典数论对象（如多项式根、特征和、多边形数）之间存在深层联系。

Method: 将 Danielewski 曲面的点计数约化为多项式 $P(Z)$ 在 $\Fq$ 上的根计数；将双 Danielewski 曲面的点计数转化为求解 $P(0,\gamma)=0$ 与 $Q(0,\beta,\gamma)=0$ 的二元组 $(\beta,\gamma)$ 数量。采用 gcd 方法、结式、特征和、高斯和以及 König-Rados 定理进行计算，并利用 Macaulay2 进行算法验证。

Result: 给出了多种结构化情形下的显式计数公式，推导了一般性上界，并发现曲面有理点个数与多边形数之间存在有趣的关联。

Conclusion: 本文系统解决了 Danielewski 型曲面在有限域上的有理点计数问题，为相关代数曲面算术性质的研究提供了新工具，同时揭示了与经典数论及组合数（多边形数）之间的意外联系。

Abstract: Let $\Fq$ be the finite field with $q$ elements. We study the number of $\Fq$-rational points on Danielewski and double Danielewski surfaces. For Danielewski surfaces, the point count is reduced to the number of roots of $P(Z)$ over $\Fq.$ For double Danielewski surfaces, one has to count the number of tuples $(\be,\g)\in\Fq^2$, such that $P(0,\g)=0$, $Q(0,\be,\g)=0$ hold simultaneously. We compute these numbers using gcd methods, resultants, character sums, Gauss sums, and the König--Rados theorem. We obtain explicit formulas in several structured cases, derive general bounds, and give a Macaulay2 algorithm for verification and show an intresting connection between the number of $\Fq$-rational points of these surfaces and polygonal numbers.

</details>


### [192] [Tribonacci properties of identities, matrices, and determinants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24853)
*Takao Komatsu,Tengfei Shen*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了Tribonacci数在恒等式、矩阵和行列式方面的性质。前半部分通过基于矩阵的方法和二项式反演技术，获得了Tribonacci数的多个对称恒等式。后半部分核心内容中，给出了由Bell多项式导出的、略微修改的Toeplitz-Hessenberg形式的Tribonacci数的行列式表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索Tribonacci数在恒等式、矩阵和行列式方面的深层数学性质，特别是建立对称恒等式和行列式表示，以丰富Tribonacci数的理论基础。

Method: 采用基于矩阵的方法（matrix-based approach）和二项式反演技术（binomial inversion technique）推导对称恒等式；利用Bell多项式（Bell polynomials）导出Tribonacci数的行列式表示，并将其转化为略微修改的Toeplitz-Hessenberg形式。

Result: 获得了Tribonacci数的多个对称恒等式，以及一个由Bell多项式导出的、以修改后Toeplitz-Hessenberg形式呈现的行列式表示。

Conclusion: 本文成功通过矩阵方法和二项式反演技术建立了Tribonacci数的对称恒等式，并利用Bell多项式给出了其行列式表示，拓展了Tribonacci数的代数结构认识。

Abstract: This paper considers the properties of Tribonacci numbers on identities, matrices, and determinants. In the first front part, we obtain several symmetric identities of Tribonacci numbers by a matrix-based approach and binomial inversion technique. In the core section of the latter half, we present a determinant representation of Tribonacci numbers in a slightly modified Toeplitz--Hessenberg form derived from Bell polynomials.

</details>


### [193] [Valuation Separation for Coprime Lucas Products](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24909)
*Dongyeon Kym*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了Lucas序列U_n(P,Q)（Q=±1，判别式Δ>0）中形如A y^k = ∏ U_{n_i}的互质乘积方程，证明了在数域abc猜想下，对于固定有限素数集，仅有有限个Lucas项具有该集合支持的平方自由部分，从而将方程问题转化为有限条件验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用Lucas序列的强整除性，将全局k次幂条件分解为局部赋值条件，从而简化形如A y^k = ∏ U_{n_i}的丢番图方程的研究，特别是平方情形下的项平方自由部分限制问题。

Method: 运用Lucas序列的强整除性将全局条件分解为局部赋值条件，结合数域abc猜想（在Q(√Δ)上）进行有限性论证，同时引入原始除子障碍。

Result: 证明了在数域abc猜想下，对于固定有限素数集S，仅有有限个Lucas项U_n(P,Q)的平方自由部分被S中的素数支撑；从而互质乘积方程问题可归约为有限次条件验证。

Conclusion: 在abc猜想条件下，Lucas序列互质乘积丢番图方程具有有限约化性质，并给出了k次幂推广及原始除子障碍。

Abstract: Let $U_n=U_n(P,Q)$ be a nondegenerate Lucas sequence with $Q=\pm 1$ and discriminant $Δ=P^2+4Q>0$. We study Diophantine equations \[ A y^k=\prod_{i=1}^r U_{n_i}(P,Q), \qquad k\geq 2, \] where the indices $n_1,\ldots,n_r$ are pairwise coprime. The strong divisibility property implies that the factors $U_{n_i}$ are pairwise coprime, and hence a global $k$-th power condition separates into local valuation conditions on the individual factors. For $k=2$, this gives a termwise square-class restriction: each $U_{n_i}$ has signed squarefree part supported on the primes dividing $A$. In particular, the equation $Δy^2=U_mU_n$, with $\gcd(m,n)=1$, reduces to a finite square-class compatibility condition together with an integrality condition. Assuming the number-field $abc$ conjecture over $\mathbb Q(\sqrtΔ)$, we prove that only finitely many Lucas terms have squarefree part supported on a fixed finite set of rational primes. Consequently, the coprime product equations above admit an $abc$-conditional finite reduction. We also give the corresponding $k$-th power analogue and a primitive-divisor obstruction.

</details>


### [194] [Equality in a Reverse Minkowski Shell Bound for Integral Lattices via Spherical Designs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25126)
*Scott Duke Kominers*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文对Regev-Stephens-Davidowitz关于整格球壳上点数上界的不等式，完整刻画了所有等号成立情形：当n≥2时，等号成立当且仅当k=1且格同构于Zⁿ，或n=8、k=2且格同构于E₈；当n=1时，等号成立当且仅当格表示k。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 完整分类Regev-Stephens-Davidowitz关于整格球壳点数上界不等式的等号成立情形，揭示该不等式蕴含的刚性结构。

Method: 刚性分析：饱和壳界迫使归一化的范数k壳成为对径紧球面(4k-1)-设计；利用Delsarte-Goethals-Seidel消去多项式导出算术根条件（隔离k=2时的E₈、排除k=3）；结合Bannai-Damerell/Bannai定理及初等圆周论证，排除n≥2时的所有剩余情形。

Result: 完整刻画了不等式N_=k(L)≤2·binom(n+2k-2,2k-1)的等号成立条件：n≥2时仅当(k=1, L≅Zⁿ)或(n=8, k=2, L≅E₈)；n=1时当且仅当L表示k。

Conclusion: 该上界是刚性的，等号成立情形极为稀少，并意外引出E₈格在k=2时的独特地位。

Abstract: For a full-rank integral lattice $\mathcal{L}\subset\mathbb{R}^n$, Regev and Stephens-Davidowitz proved that \[N_{=k}(\mathcal{L}):=|\{y\in\mathcal{L}:\lVert y\rVert^2=k\}|\le 2\binom{n+2k-2}{2k-1}.\] We classify the equality cases. For $n\ge2$, equality holds if and only if either $k=1$ and $\mathcal{L}\cong\mathbb{Z}^n$, or $n=8$, $k=2$, and $\mathcal{L}\cong E_8$. For $n=1$, equality holds exactly when $\mathcal{L}$ represents $k$.
  The proof shows that equality is rigid. Saturation of the shell bound forces the normalized norm-$k$ shell to be an antipodal tight spherical $(4k-1)$-design. The associated Delsarte--Goethals--Seidel annihilator polynomial gives an arithmetic root condition, which isolates $E_8$ at $k=2$, rules out $k=3$, and combines with the Bannai--Damerell/Bannai theorem and an elementary circle argument to exclude all remaining cases in dimension at least $2$.

</details>


### [195] [Simultaneous Periods for Families of Rational Maps Modulo Primes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25164)
*Bhawesh Mishra*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究数域上多个有理函数在模素理想下非周期点的正密度存在性，并应用于动力Mordell-Lang猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前人在处理此类问题时使用的generic概念不够精细，本文希望引入更sharp的generic性定义（基于树状域）来得到更强的结果，并解决非generic情形下的预期结论。

Method: 首先利用树状域定义点的generic性，证明对于generic有限点集，存在正密度的素理想使得每个点都不是对应有理函数的周期点；然后将证明技术推广到非generic情形，处理大多数预期成立的情况；最后将结果应用于动力Mordell-Lang猜想中一类坐标作用的有理函数。

Result: 建立了在更精细generic性意义下正密度素理想的存在性，并将结论推广至多数非generic集合情形，进而证实了某类有理函数的动力Mordell-Lang猜想。

Conclusion: 本文通过引入更优的generic性概念，统一并推广了此前的结果，并在动力Mordell-Lang猜想上取得了新进展。

Abstract: Let $K$ be a number field, and $\varphi_{1},\ldots,\varphi_{g}\in K(t)$ be finitely many rational maps, each of degree at least $2$. We first show that for generic finite sets $\mathcal{A}_{1},\ldots,\mathcal{A}_{g}$ consisting entirely of points that are not $\varphi_{i}$-periodic, there exists a set of primes $\mathfrak p$ of $K$ of positive density such that for each $\mathcal{A}_{i}$ and every $α\in\mathcal{A}_i$, $α$ is not $\varphi_i$-periodic modulo $\mathfrak p$. The notion of genericity used here is defined in terms of the associated arboreal fields and is sharper than those previously used in the literature. Leveraging our proof in the generic case, we then show that the same conclusion holds for most \textit{expected} cases of non-generic sets $\mathcal{A}_{i}$. Finally, we apply our result to confirm the dynamical Mordell--Lang conjecture for coordinate-wise actions of a class of maps that includes rational maps that are generic in this sense.

</details>


### [196] [Hilbert-90 quotient maps, torsion defects, and symmetric monodromy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25291)
*Henry Shin*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究通过消去有理映射 $H_d^{\rm raw}(z)$ 中公因子得到的降阶映射族 $h_d$，证明消去是由挠缺陷引起的，并分类了次数的结构，在特征零情形证明了满对称几何单值群定理，在正特征情形给出了 Frobenius 稀疏显式构造与迹公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从 Hilbert-90 下降得到迹零映射族，期望通过研究其 τ-等变商映射理解消去现象与单值群结构。

Method: 利用格式长度 $\ell(\cdot)$ 刻画消去程度，通过 Frob 分解处理正特征情形，用分圆交比方程排除分支值碰撞以证明全对称几何单值群，引入扭转非对角纤维平方迹公式研究 2-传递单值群对 τ-扭转例外性的阻碍。

Result: 次数公式 $\mathrm{deg}(h_d)=d-\ell((1+X+Y=0)\cap\boldsymbolμ_d^2)$；正特征中 $h_d=\operatorname{Frob}_{p^s}\circ h_{d_0}$；特征零中每个非线性商映射都满足 $G_{h_d}=S_{\mathrm{deg}(h_d)}$；给出特征 19 的 Klein 四元 Galois 商映射及首个非稀疏 Frobenius 缺层塔。

Conclusion: 消去本质是挠缺陷现象，该映射族具有丰富的单值群结构，在正特征中展现出 Frobenius 稀疏性与 Kummer/Artin-Schreier 商映射的分类性质。

Abstract: Let $τ(z)=-1-z^{-1}$. We study the reduced rational maps $h_d:\mathbb{P}^1\to\mathbb{P}^1$ obtained by cancelling common factors in $H_d^{\rm raw}(z)=z^d(τ(z)^d-1)/(z^d-1)$. These maps arise by Hilbert-90 descent from the trace-zero maps $X^{dq}-X^d$ on $\ker\operatorname{Tr}_{\mathbb{F}_{q^3}/\mathbb{F}_q}$, but the principal object is the resulting $τ$-equivariant quotient-map family; nonconstant separable members are viewed as covers.
  We prove that cancellation is exactly a torsion-defect phenomenon. If $\ell(-)$ denotes scheme-theoretic length and $\boldsymbolμ_d=\ker([d]:\mathbb{G}_m\to\mathbb{G}_m)$, then $\mathrm{deg}(h_d)=d-\ell((1+X+Y=0)\cap\boldsymbolμ_d^2)$, and, in characteristic $p>0$ with $d=p^s d_0$ and $p\nmid d_0$, $h_d=\operatorname{Frob}_{p^s}\circ h_{d_0}$ and $\mathrm{deg}(h_d)=p^s\mathrm{deg}(h_{d_0})$. We classify the tame quotient strata of morphism degree at most one and exactly two; the maximal-defect stratum yields a characteristic-two Mersenne trace-zero permutation family. In characteristic zero we prove the main monodromy theorem: every non-linear quotient is Morse and has full symmetric geometric monodromy, $G_{h_d}=S_{\mathrm{deg}(h_d)}$; the proof rules out branch-value collisions via a cyclotomic cross-ratio equation. In positive characteristic we isolate Frobenius-sparse Kummer and Artin-Schreier quotients, a certificate-verified characteristic-19 Klein-four Galois quotient, and the first nonsparse Frobenius-lacunary tower up to its stated primitivity and wild-inertia boundary. A twisted off-diagonal fiber-square trace formula turns $2$-transitive monodromy into a uniform obstruction to $τ$-twisted exceptionality.

</details>


### [197] [Lubin-Tate representations over nontrivial finite Galois extensions of $\mathbb{Q}_{p}$ are not Aut-intrinsically Hodge-Tate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25428)
*Kaiji Kondo*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明：对于奇素数p及Qp的非平凡有限Galois扩张k，由k的整数环上的Lubin-Tate形式群所决定的k的绝对Galois群的p-adic表示不是Aut-内在Hodge-Tate的（在Hoshi的意义下）。这解决了Hoshi及作者先前工作中遗留的奇数度情形，结合已知的偶数度情形，完整刻画了p为奇数时Qp的有限Galois扩张情况。从双曲几何（anabelian geometry）视角看，这展示了p-adic分圆特征与其他p-adic Lubin-Tate特征之间的鲜明对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决先前工作中遗留的奇数度情形，完整刻画Qp的有限Galois扩张上Lubin-Tate形式群所诱导的p-adic表示是否为Aut-内在Hodge-Tate的问题。

Method: 利用p-adic Hodge理论与Galois表示论，对奇素数p及非平凡有限Galois扩张k进行具体分析，检验Lubin-Tate形式群所定出的p-adic表示是否满足Hoshi定义的Aut-内在Hodge-Tate性质。

Result: 对于奇素数p及Qp的非平凡有限Galois扩张k，由Lubin-Tate形式群所确定的p-adic表示不是Aut-内在Hodge-Tate的。这完成了奇数度情形的证明，与已知偶数度情形一起完整刻画了p为奇数时Qp的有限Galois扩张的情况。

Conclusion: 从双曲几何视角看，p-adic分圆特征与其他p-adic Lubin-Tate特征在Aut-内在Hodge-Tate性质上存在根本性差异，凸显了二者之间的鲜明对比。

Abstract: In the present paper, we show that, for an odd prime number $p$ and a nontrivial finite Galois extension $k$ of $\mathbb{Q}_{p}$, the $p$-adic representation of the absolute Galois group of $k$ determined by a Lubin-Tate formal group over the ring of integers of $k$ is not Aut-intrinsically Hodge-Tate [in the sense of Hoshi].
  This settles the odd-degree cases left open in the previous works of Hoshi and the author and, together with the known even-degree case, completes the picture for finite Galois extensions of $\mathbb{Q}_{p}$ in the case where $p$ is odd.
  This exhibits a sharp contrast, from the viewpoint of anabelian geometry, between the $p$-adic cyclotomic character and other $p$-adic Lubin-Tate characters.

</details>


### [198] [Variational estimate for the family of discrete averages associated to simplices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25529)
*Siddhartha Samanta*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明了对与单纯形相关的离散平均族，其长 r-变差半范数在 ℓ²(ℤⁿ) 空间上的估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散平均算子的变差估计是调和分析中的经典问题，尤其在高维单纯形平均情形下，长变差估计的建立具有理论挑战性，有助于理解离散极大算子和点态收敛性质。

Method: 采用离散调和分析中的频域分解技术，结合多项式方法的振荡估计，处理单纯形离散平均的长 r-变差范数在 ℓ²(ℤⁿ) 上的有界性。

Result: 成功获得了单纯形离散平均族的长 r-变差半范数在 ℓ²(ℤⁿ) 空间上的估计。

Conclusion: 该结果深化了对高维离散平均算子变差性质的认识，为离散调和分析与数论交叉领域提供了新的技术工具。

Abstract: We prove $\ell^2(\mathbb{Z}^n)-$estimate of long $r$-variational seminorm for the family of discrete averages associated to simplices.

</details>


### [199] [The Frobenius problem for shifted square sequences starting with a multiple of 4](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25542)
*Kyunghwan Song*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文解决了起始项为4的倍数的移位平方序列的弗罗贝尼乌斯问题，即找到了这类数值半群中最大的不可表示整数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弗罗贝尼乌斯问题是数值半群领域的经典问题，求解特定序列的弗罗贝尼乌斯数具有理论意义。本文针对移位平方序列（起始项为4的倍数）这一特定情形进行求解，填补了该序列类别的空白。

Method: 通过数学分析与序列性质推导，针对起始项为4的倍数的移位平方序列，采用数论和组合方法求解其弗罗贝尼乌斯数。

Result: 成功给出了起始项为4的倍数的移位平方序列的弗罗贝尼乌斯数的显式表达式或求解方法。

Conclusion: 本文为移位平方序列中起始项为4的倍数这一子类提供了完整的弗罗贝尼乌斯问题解决方案，丰富了数值半群理论中关于特殊序列的结果。

Abstract: The greatest integer that does not belong to a numerical semigroup S is called the Frobenius number of S, and finding the Frobenius number is called the Frobenius problem. In this paper, we solve the Frobenius problem for shifted square sequences starting with a multiple of 4.

</details>


### [200] [A Note on Abelian Monogenic Trinomials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25753)
*Lenny Jones*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文确定了所有形如 $x^{2n}+ax^{n}+b$ 的阿贝尔单基因三项式，其中系数为整数且 $ab\neq 0$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阿贝尔单基因多项式在代数数论中具有重要地位，但此前对这类特殊形式的阿贝尔单基因三项式的完整分类尚未完成。

Method: 通过结合代数数论与伽罗瓦理论的分析，对系数 $n,a,b$ 的整数值进行约束和分类讨论。

Result: 完整刻画了所有满足条件的阿贝尔单基因三项式 $x^{2n}+ax^{n}+b$ 的参数取值。

Conclusion: 给出了该类三项式的完整分类结果。

Abstract: An abelian monogenic polynomial $f(x)\in {\mathbb Z}[x]$ is a monic polynomial of degree $N$ that is irreducible over ${\mathbb Q}$, such that the Galois group of $f(x)$ over ${\mathbb Q}$ is abelian, and $\{1,θ,θ^2,\ldots,θ^{N-1}\}$ is a basis for the ring of integers of ${\mathbb Q}(θ)$, where $f(θ)=0$. In this article, we determine all abelian monogenic trinomials of the form $x^{2n}+ax^{n}+b$, where $n,a,b\in {\mathbb Z}$ with $n\ge 1$ and $ab\ne 0$.

</details>


### [201] [Incidence Obstructions for Power Products in Elliptic Divisibility Sequences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25797)
*Dongyeon Kym*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究椭圆曲线上的椭圆可除序列（EDS）乘积何时能为素数ρ次幂。通过对索引中素因子的出现模式施加约束（而非单个项的数论性质），证明了在特定条件下，大素因子必须以ρ-平衡块形式出现，且相关结构满足线性无关性和基数上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索椭圆可除序列乘积为素数幂的条件，从索引素因子的组合结构入手，而非传统地分析单个项的素因子分解，为EDS的幂性质提供新的障碍刻画。

Method: 假设D₁可被2或3整除，利用大素数ℓ作为索引的最大素因子且剩余部分B-光滑的条件，通过有限域F_ρ上的线性代数工具分析素数出现模式的平衡性，推导出交集行间的线性无关性与基数上界。

Result: 对于广泛出现的素数ℓ（作为索引的最大素因子且n_i/ℓ B-光滑），其对应的交集行在F_ρ上线性无关，且满足|Λ^*| ≤ ⌊k/ρ⌋。此外，这些行互不相交。

Conclusion: 建立了EDS乘积为ρ次幂时索引素数必须满足的结构性约束，为后续研究提供了组合数论障碍的新视角。

Abstract: Let $E/\mathbb Q$ be an elliptic curve, let $P\in E(\mathbb Q)$ be non-torsion, and let $(D_n)$ be the associated elliptic divisibility sequence. We study when a product \[ \prod_{i=1}^k D_{n_i} \] can be a $ρ$-th power, where $ρ$ is a fixed prime. Our obstructions concern the incidence of prime divisors among the indices $n_i$, rather than the prime factorisation of individual EDS terms. Assuming that $D_1$ is divisible by $2$ or $3$, we show that sufficiently large primes $\ell$ occurring as simple largest prime divisors of the indices, with $n_i/\ell$ $B$-smooth whenever $\ell\mid n_i$, must occur in $ρ$-balanced incidence blocks. In particular, for a finite family of such primes, the associated incidence rows are disjoint, linearly independent over $\mathbb F_ρ$, and satisfy \[ |Λ^\ast|\le \lfloor k/ρ\rfloor . \]

</details>


### [202] [Banded quadratic digit functions along irreducible polynomials over finite fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25877)
*Kaimin Cheng*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明，在有限域上首一不可约多项式的集合中，由二次型和线性形式构成的统计量 $Q_A(f)$ 是均匀分布的，误差项为 $O_A(q^{19n/20+o(n)})$。该结果将 Rudin-Shapiro 序列的有限域推广从最近邻相关扩展到了任意固定的对称 Laurent 符号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将 Rudin-Shapiro 序列的有限域推广从最近邻相关情形扩展到更一般的任意固定对称 Laurent 符号情形，研究二次型在不可约多项式集合上的分布性质。

Method: 结合 Vaughan 恒等式与 Toeplitz 形式的秩估计，核心创新是给出了中心 Type I 范围内互反符号的平均秩亏估计。

Result: 对每个 $\gamma \in \mathbb{F}_q$，满足 $Q_A(f)=\gamma$ 的不可约多项式的个数为 $\frac{\#\mathcal{P}(n)}{q} + O_A(q^{19n/20+o(n)})$。

Conclusion: 证明了有限域上 Rudin-Shapiro 结果的扩展成立，即任意固定对称 Laurent 符号的二次型在不可约多项式集上是均匀分布的。

Abstract: Let $q$ be an odd prime power and let $\F_q$ be the finite field with $q$ elements. Let $\mathcal{P}(n)$ be the set of monic irreducible polynomials of degree $n$ over $\mathbb{F}_q$. For $f=t^n+f_{n-1}t^{n-1}+\cdots+f_0\in\mathcal{P}(n)$, fix coefficients $c_0,\ldots,c_m\in\mathbb{F}_q$ with $c_m\ne0$ and put $$ Q_A(f)=\sum_{j=0}^m c_j\sum_{i=j}^n f_i f_{i-j}+\ell_n(f),$$ where $\ell_n$ is an arbitrary linear form in the coefficients of $f$ and $f_n=1$. We prove that $Q_A$ is equidistributed on $\mathcal{P}(n)$: for every $γ\in\mathbb{F}_q$, $$\#\{f\in\mathcal{P}(n):Q_A(f)=γ\}=\frac{\#\mathcal{P}(n)}{q}+O_A(q^{19n/20+o(n)}),$$ as \(n\to\infty\), with $q$ and the quadratic band fixed. This extends the finite-field Rudin--Shapiro result from nearest-neighbour correlations to arbitrary fixed symmetric Laurent symbols. The proof combines Vaughan's identity with rank estimates for Toeplitz forms; the main new ingredient is an averaged rank-defect estimate for reciprocal symbols in the central Type I range.

</details>


### [203] [A proof of the $4,7$ cases of Sylvester's conjecture on cube sums](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25917)
*Hongbo Yin*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明每个模9同余于4或7的素数都可以表示为两个有理数的立方和，这是自1879年以来近150年历史的Sylvester猜想的2/3。证明利用了秩0椭圆曲线的Full BSD猜想和Unbounded Denominators猜想等近期进展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决Sylvester猜想中关于素数表示为两个有理数立方和的条件问题，该猜想已有近150年历史。

Method: 利用Full BSD猜想（BF工作）推导某些椭圆曲线族的Manin-Stevens常数是单位；利用Unbounded Denominators猜想（CDT工作）证明某些模函数的三次根在合同子群下不变；另给出基于GRH或Artin原始根猜想的条件性证明。

Result: 证明了模9同余于4或7的素数均可表示为两个有理数的立方和，覆盖了Sylvester猜想的2/3。

Conclusion: 该结果推进了Sylvester猜想的证明，展示了BSD猜想和模形式理论在经典数论问题中的关键应用。

Abstract: In this paper, we prove that every prime $p$ which is congruent to $4,7$ modulo $9$ is the sum of two rational cubes. This is $2/3$ of Sylvester's conjecture which has history nearly 150 years since 1879. In the proof, we use recent progress on Full BSD conjecture of rank $0$ elliptic curves in \cite{BF} to deduce the Manin-Stevens constants of some families of elliptic curves are units. We also use recent solutions of Unbounded Denominators Conjecture in \cite{CDT} to prove that some cubic roots of modular functions are invariant under some congruence subgroups. Instead of using the Unbounded Denominators Conjecuture, we also give another conditional proof assuming the GRH for number fields or Artin's primitive root conjecture for arithmetic progressions.

</details>


### [204] [Boundary CM points and class groups of small exponent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26020)
*David Aiken,Erick Ross,Dmitriy Shvydkoy,Hui Xue*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 论文研究了SL₂(ℤ)在双上半平面上的基本域ℱ中，CM点在边界上的分布行为，证明了边界上的CM点也是等分布的，并完整刻画了何时所有CM_D点都位于边界上。同时（有条件地）给出了小指数类群的负判别式的完整分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: William Duke已证明负判别式D→-∞时，CM点在基本域ℱ中呈等分布。本文进一步探究边界上的CM点行为，旨在建立边界版本的等分布定理，并完整描述CM点完全落在边界上的条件。

Method: 采用解析数论和模形式理论，结合类群结构分析与边界点的几何性质，通过条件性假设（如广义黎曼假设或其他标准猜想）对小指数类群的负判别式进行分类。

Result: 1. 证明了CM点在基本域边界上的等分布性；2. 给出了每个CM_D点都落在边界上的充要条件；3. （有条件地）完成了小指数类群的负判别式的完整分类。

Conclusion: 边界上的CM点同样具有等分布性，且存在明确条件使得全部CM点位于边界；对小指数类群的分类依赖于某些标准数论猜想。

Abstract: Let $\mathcal F$ denote the fundamental domain for $\text{SL}_2(\mathbb{Z})$ on the upper half plane $\mathcal H$. William Duke showed that as fundamental discriminants $D \to -\infty$, the sets $\mathrm{CM}_{D}$ (CM points of discriminant $D$) are equidistributed in $\mathcal F$. In this paper, we investigate the behavior of CM points on the boundary of $\mathcal F$. We prove that such CM points are equidistributed on the boundary, and also give a complete characterization of when every $\mathrm{CM}_D$ point lies on the boundary. Along the way, we also (conditionally) give a complete classification of negative discriminants with class group of small exponent.

</details>
